ConnectApp hendelsesflyt

En linjefestet guide til opprettelse, detaljer, redigering, sletting og avlysning.

1. Brukeren åpner siden1.1 Script-rekkefølgen bygger appflaten1.2 Firebase oppretter auth- og Firestore-håndtak1.3 Sesjonshjelpere definerer gjeldende bruker og leilighetskontekst1.4 Dashboard-siden verifiserer avhengigheter og monterer hendelses-UI1.5 Første sesjon starter live-abonnementer og første render1.6 Tilgjengelige hendelsesspørringer bestemmer hva som kan rendres2. Brukeren åpner «opprett ny hendelse»2.1 Dashboardet renderer opprett-valg2.2 Datoaktivering kaller opprett-callbacken2.3 Innebygd hendelsesoverlay monterer opprett-skjemaet2.4 Core åpner og nullstiller opprett-overlayet2.5 Skjemamarkup og interaksjonshåndterere finnes før innsending3. Brukeren oppretter hendelsen3.1 Innsending starter validering3.2 Sesjon og fellesromtilgjengelighet sjekkes3.3 Datahjelper bygger målgruppe og skriver Firestore-dokumenter3.4 Sikkerhetsregler validerer opprett-form og invitasjonstilgang3.5 Suksess oppdaterer UI og dashboard-tilstand3.6 Rombestillingsreview kan senere godkjenne/avslå koblede hendelser4. Brukeren åpner hendelsesdetaljer4.1 Hendelsesklikk velger hendelsen4.2 Deltaker- og samlingsdata normaliseres4.3 Detalj-UI renderer status, deltakere og handlinger4.4 Live-oppdateringer holder åpen detaljvisning oppdatert5. Brukeren redigerer en hendelse5.1 Detaljhandlinger kobler redigerings-callbacks5.2 Redigeringsskjemaet forhåndsutfylles fra hendelsesdata5.3 Lagre validerer og bygger update-payload5.4 Firestore-oppdatering synker hendelse og invitasjonsgraf5.5 Vellykket redigering går tilbake til renderede detaljer6. Brukeren sletter en hendelse6.1 Slettehandling vises bare når det er tillatt6.2 Bekreftelse utfører sletteforespørselen6.3 Datahjelper sletter hendelsen og koblede dokumenter6.4 Dashboardet rydder valgt tilstand og re-render7. Brukeren avlyser en hendelse7.1 Avlysningshandling rendres separat fra sletting7.2 Bekreftelse utfører avlysningsforespørsel7.3 Datahjelper oppdaterer avlysningsstatus7.4 Avlyst tilstand re-rendrer i liste og detaljer

Hendelsesflyt, fra sidelasting til avlysning

Dette er en personlig kodebaseguide: hvert viktige overleveringspunkt vises som trigger, kjede, kobling, eksakt kildeutdrag og dataflyt. Kodeblokkene har linjenummer i venstre marg; kildeteksten etter linjenummeret er hentet direkte fra repoet.

Appen bruker Firebase/Firestore i stedet for vanlige REST-endepunkter, så «API-kallene» under er Firestore-dokumentstier som events/{eventId}, events/{eventId}/attendees/{attendeeId}, eventInvites/{inviteId} og bookingRequests/{requestId}.

filstier funksjoner / koblinger data / props / state
Lesetips: følg hver oransje kobling som «neste hopp». Den lilla etiketten i hver kodeoverskrift peker på linjen eller kallet som fører deg videre.
Hendelsesflyt

1. Brukeren åpner siden

Dette er den første brikken: HTML-en laster script, Firebase initialiseres, og dashboard-modulen eier første render og live-abonnementene for hendelser.

1.1 Script-rekkefølgen bygger appflaten

① Trigger: Nettleseren parser `public/menu2.html`; defer-script kjører i dokumentrekkefølge etter at HTML-en er parset.
② Kjeden:

Dataflyt

window.firebase/app/firebase-auth/firebase-firestore lastes før appkoden.

window.connectSession, window.connectData, window.eventFormUi og window.eventDetailsUi blir den delte avhengighetsflaten.

1.2 Firebase oppretter auth- og Firestore-håndtak

① Trigger: `app/firebase.js` kjører under bootstrap.
② Kjeden:
public/scripts/app/firebase.js:1-39window.firebaseAuth og window.firebaseDb eksporteres
// public/scripts/app/firebase.js:1-39
   1  // Author: Sondre
   2  // Shared Firebase startup for browser pages.
   3  //
   4  // This file keeps Firebase initialization in one place so page scripts can use
   5  // the same auth and Firestore handles without repeating setup logic.
   6  (function initializeConnectFirebase() {
   7  	// The local firebase-config.js file is expected to define window.firebaseConfig.
   8  	// Keeping that config in a separate file makes it easier to swap projects without
   9  	// rewriting the shared browser startup code.
  10  	// The config is placed on `window` so this shared bootstrap file can read it from
  11  	// any page as long as firebase-config.js loaded first.
  12  	if (!window.firebaseConfig) {
  13  		throw new Error('firebase-config.js is missing. Copy the example file and add your project config.');
  14  	}
  15  
  16  	// Reuse the same Firebase app if another page in this browser session already
  17  	// initialized it earlier.
  18  	if (!firebase.apps.length) {
  19  		firebase.initializeApp(window.firebaseConfig);
  20  	}
  21  
  22  	// Expose the small set of shared Firebase handles the rest of the client needs.
  23  	// The rest of the browser app reads from this shared namespace instead of calling
  24  	// firebase.auth() and firebase.firestore() independently in every file.
  25  	window.connectApp = window.connectApp || {};
  26  	window.connectApp.auth = firebase.auth();
  27  	window.connectApp.db = firebase.firestore();
  28  	// Firestore persistence keeps a local cache in the browser. That can reduce some
  29  	// repeat reads and makes reloads feel more resilient, but not every browser mode
  30  	// supports it, so failures are logged and then ignored safely.
  31  	if (typeof window.connectApp.db.enablePersistence === 'function') {
  32  		window.connectApp.db.enablePersistence({ synchronizeTabs: true }).catch(function (error) {
  33  			console.warn('Firestore persistence is unavailable for this browser session.', error?.code || error?.message || error);
  34  		});
  35  	}
  36  	// Expose the Firestore server timestamp helper once so every data-layer file can
  37  	// use the same source of truth for createdAt and updatedAt fields.
  38  	window.connectApp.serverTimestamp = firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp;
  39  })();
eksporterer window.firebaseAuth / window.firebaseDb
Hvorfor dette betyr noe: Alle hendelseslesinger og -skrivinger senere i kjeden bruker Firestore-håndtaket som opprettes her.

Dataflyt

firebaseConfig initialiserer Firebase-appen.

window.firebaseDb er databaseobjektet som sendes implisitt gjennom globale hjelpere.

// public/scripts/app/session.js:229-318
 229  		const snapshot = getSessionState();

 235  	// Register one listener that wants to react to login, logout, profile loading,
 236  	// or session errors. The returned function removes that listener again.
 237  	function subscribeToSession(listener, options) {
 238  		sessionSubscribers.add(listener);
 239  
 240  		if (options?.emitImmediately !== false) {
 241  			listener(getSessionState());
 242  		}
 243  
 244  		return function unsubscribeFromSession() {
 245  			sessionSubscribers.delete(listener);
 246  		};
 247  	}
 248  
 249  	// Some flows need to wait until the shared auth/profile handshake has finished
 250  	// before they can safely decide whether the user is actually signed out or the
 251  	// profile is simply still loading.
 252  	// Returning a Promise here lets page startup code pause on the shared session
 253  	// contract instead of opening its own duplicate auth listeners.
 254  	function waitForSessionResolution() {
 255  		if (sessionState.isResolved) {
 256  			return Promise.resolve(getSessionState());
 257  		}
 258  
 259  		return new Promise(function (resolve) {
 260  			const unsubscribe = subscribeToSession(function (snapshot) {
 261  				if (!snapshot.isResolved) {
 262  					return;
 263  				}
 264  
 265  				unsubscribe();
 266  				resolve(snapshot);
 267  			}, {
 268  				emitImmediately: true
 269  			});
 270  		});
 271  	}
 272  
 273  	// Read the current user's profile document without mutating it.
 274  	// Page files use this when they only need to inspect access or role state.
 275  	// Because this is async, the browser can keep responding while Firestore loads.
 276  	async function loadUserProfile(user) {
 277  		if (!user) {
 278  			return null;
 279  		}
 280  
 281  		const profileSnapshot = await db.collection('users').doc(user.uid).get();
 282  		if (!profileSnapshot.exists) {
 283  			return null;
 284  		}
 285  
 286  		return {
 287  			uid: user.uid,
 288  			...profileSnapshot.data()
 289  		};
 290  	}
 291  
 292  	// Keep login redirection in one helper so pages do not each hardcode their own
 293  	// slightly different redirect behavior.
 294  	function redirectToLogin() {
 295  		window.location.href = 'index.html';
 296  	}
 297  
 298  	// Some pages require a signed-in user before they can even render safely.
 299  	// Returning null after redirect lets the caller stop its startup flow cleanly.
 300  	function requireSignedInUser(user) {
 301  		if (!user) {
 302  			redirectToLogin();
 303  			return null;
 304  		}
 305  
 306  		return user;
 307  	}
 308  
 309  	// Make sure every signed-in user has the minimum profile shape the rest of the
 310  	// app expects before chat, dashboard, and role-specific screens load.
 311  	async function ensureUserProfile(user, options) {
 312  		if (!user) {
 313  			return null;
 314  		}
 315  
 316  		// Some flows want to wait for the explicit registration write instead of
 317  		// allowing this helper to create a fallback profile immediately.
 318  		const allowCreateFallback = options?.allowCreateFallback !== false;

Hvorfor dette betyr noe: Hendelser avgrenses av leilighet og rettigheter; uten dette sesjonslaget ville ikke spørringene vite hvilke hendelser brukeren kan se.

Dataflyt

currentAuthUser flyter inn i eierskap og invitasjonssjekker.

currentApartmentId flyter inn i alle hendelsesspørringer og payloads.

public/scripts/features/dashboard/dashboard-workspace.js:1245-1455subscribeToEventUpdates() kobler på Firestore-lyttere
// public/scripts/features/dashboard/dashboard-workspace.js:1245-1455
1245      function subscribeToEventUpdates(options) {
1246        const { db, user, userProfile, onChanged } = options;
1247  
1248        let debounceTimer = null;
1249      const visibleSnapshots = new Map();
1250      let ownEventsById = new Map();
1251      let inviteRecordsById = new Map();
1252      const inviteEventDocsById = new Map();
1253      const inviteEventUnsubs = new Map();
1254  
1255      function syncInviteEventSubscriptions() {
1256        const inviteVisibleEventIds = new Set([...inviteRecordsById.values()].reduce(function (result, inviteRecord) {
1257          const eventId = String(inviteRecord?.eventId || '');
1258          if (inviteRecord?.audienceMode === 'inviteOnly' && inviteRecord?.eventStatus === 'visible' && eventId) {
1259            result.push(eventId);
1260          }
1261          return result;
1262        }, []));
1263  
1264        inviteEventUnsubs.forEach(function (unsubscribe, eventId) {
1265          if (inviteVisibleEventIds.has(eventId)) {
1266            return;
1267          }
1268  
1269          unsubscribe();
1270          inviteEventUnsubs.delete(eventId);
1271          inviteEventDocsById.delete(eventId);
1272        });
1273  
1274        inviteVisibleEventIds.forEach(function (eventId) {
1275          if (inviteEventUnsubs.has(eventId)) {
1276            return;
1277          }
1278  
1279          const unsubscribe = db.collection('events').doc(eventId).onSnapshot(function (snapshot) {
1280            if (snapshot.exists) {
1281              inviteEventDocsById.set(eventId, eventDataApi.normalizeEventRecord({ id: snapshot.id, ...snapshot.data() }));
1282            } else {
1283              inviteEventDocsById.delete(eventId);
1284            }
1285            scheduleRefresh();
1286          }, function () {
1287            inviteEventDocsById.delete(eventId);
1288            scheduleRefresh();
1289          });
1290  
1291          inviteEventUnsubs.set(eventId, unsubscribe);
1292        });
1293      }
1294  
1295      function buildLiveEventCollections() {
1296        const rawVisibleEvents = [...visibleSnapshots.values()].flatMap(function (snapshot) {
1297          return snapshot.docs.map(function (doc) {
1298            return eventDataApi.normalizeEventRecord({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() });
1299          });
1300        });
1301        const invitedEvents = eventDataApi.buildUniqueEventList([...inviteEventDocsById.values()]).filter(function (event) {
1302          return eventDataApi.canUserAccessVisibleEvent(event, {
1303            userId: user.uid,
1304            userProfile
1305          });
1306        });
1307        const visibleEvents = eventDataApi.buildUniqueEventList(rawVisibleEvents.concat(invitedEvents)).filter(function (event) {
1308          return eventDataApi.canUserAccessVisibleEvent(event, {
1309            userId: user.uid,
1310            userProfile
1311          });
1312        });
1313        const ownEvents = eventDataApi.sortEventsByDateTime(eventDataApi.buildUniqueEventList([
1314          ...ownEventsById.values(),
1315          ...optimisticOwnEventsById.values()
1316        ]));
1317        const joinedEvents = eventDataApi.sortEventsByDateTime(visibleEvents.filter(function (event) {
1318          return joinedEventIds.has(event.id);
1319        }));
1320  
1321        return {
1322          visibleEvents,
1323          ownEvents,
1324          joinedEventIds: new Set(joinedEventIds),
1325          joinedEvents,
1326          calendarEvents: eventDataApi.buildUniqueEventList([...ownEvents, ...joinedEvents, ...invitedEvents]),
1327          inviteRecords: [...inviteRecordsById.values()]
1328        };
1329      }
1330  
1331        function scheduleRefresh() {
1332          clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
1333        debounceTimer = setTimeout(function () {
1334          const eventCollections = buildLiveEventCollections();
1335          applyEventCollections(eventCollections);
1336  
1337          if (typeof onChanged === 'function') {
1338            Promise.resolve(onChanged({
1339              visibleEvents: eventCollections.visibleEvents,
1340              ownEvents: eventCollections.ownEvents,
1341              joinedEvents: eventCollections.joinedEvents,
1342              inviteRecords: eventCollections.inviteRecords
1343            })).catch(function (error) {
1344              console.error('dashboard event subscription refresh failed:', error);
1345            });
1346          }
1347          }, 120);
1348        }
1349  
1350        const visibleUnsubs = (eventDataApi.buildAccessibleVisibleEventQueries({
1351          db,
1352          user,
1353          userProfile
1354      }) || []).map(function (query, index) {
1355        return query.onSnapshot(function (snapshot) {
1356          visibleSnapshots.set(index, snapshot);
1357          scheduleRefresh();
1358        }, function () {});
1359      });
1360  
1361        const unsubOwn = db.collection('events')
1362          .where('createdByUid', '==', user.uid)
1363  		.onSnapshot(function (snapshot) {
1364          ownEventsById = new Map();
1365          snapshot.docs.forEach(function (doc) {
1366            const rawOwnEvent = { id: doc.id, ...doc.data() };
1367            ownEventsById.set(doc.id, eventDataApi.normalizeEventRecord(rawOwnEvent));
1368  				optimisticOwnEventsById.delete(doc.id);
1369          });
1370          scheduleRefresh();
1371        }, function () {});
1372      const unsubInvites = typeof eventDataApi.subscribeUserEventInvites === 'function'
1373        ? eventDataApi.subscribeUserEventInvites({
1374          db,
1375          userId: user.uid,
1376          onChange: function (inviteRecords) {
1377            inviteRecordsById = new Map();
1378            inviteRecords.forEach(function (inviteRecord) {
1379              inviteRecordsById.set(inviteRecord.id, inviteRecord);
1380            });
1381            syncInviteEventSubscriptions();
1382            scheduleRefresh();
1383          },
1384          onError: function () {}
1385        })
1386        : null;
1387  
1388        return function unsubscribe() {
1389          clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
1390          visibleUnsubs.forEach((unsubscribe) => unsubscribe());
1391          unsubOwn();
1392        if (typeof unsubInvites === 'function') {
1393          unsubInvites();
1394        }
1395        inviteEventUnsubs.forEach(function (unsubscribe) {
1396          unsubscribe();
1397        });
1398        inviteEventUnsubs.clear();
1399        inviteEventDocsById.clear();
1400        };
1401      }
1402

1407        currentJoinedEvents = [];

1.6 Tilgjengelige hendelsesspørringer bestemmer hva som kan rendres

// public/scripts/data/events-data.js:342-389
 342  	// Build the Firestore queries needed to fetch every visible event scope a user
 343  	// is allowed to access.
 344  	function buildAccessibleVisibleEventQueries(options) {
 345  		const db = options.db;
 346  		const user = options.user || {};
 347  		const userProfile = options.userProfile || {};
 348  		const queries = [
 349  			db.collection('events')
 350  				.where('status', '==', 'visible')
 351  				.where('audienceMode', '==', 'anyone')
 352  		];
 353  		const residentTarget = buildResidentInviteTarget({
 354  			uid: user.uid,
 355  			displayName: userProfile.displayName || user.displayName || ''
 356  		});
 357  
 358  		if (sanitizeString(user.uid)) {
 359  			queries.push(
 360  				db.collection('events')
 361  					.where('status', '==', 'visible')
 362  					.where('extraInviteUids', 'array-contains', user.uid)
 363  			);
 364  		}
 365  
 366  		if (residentTarget) {
 367  			queries.push(
 368  				db.collection('events')
 369  					.where('status', '==', 'visible')
 370  					.where('visibilityTargets', 'array-contains', residentTarget)
 371  			);
 372  		}
 373  
 374  		['area', 'block', 'floor'].forEach((audienceMode) => {
 375  			const scopeTarget = buildAudienceScopeTarget(audienceMode, userProfile);
 376  			if (!scopeTarget) {
 377  				return;
 378  			}
 379  
 380  			queries.push(
 381  				db.collection('events')
 382  					.where('status', '==', 'visible')
 383  					.where('audienceMode', '==', audienceMode)
 384  					.where('audienceScopeValue', '==', scopeTarget.value)
 385  			);
 386  		});
 387  
 388  		return queries;
 389  	}

1072  	// and direct-invite data.
1073  	async function loadDashboardEventCollections(options) {
1074  		const visibleSnapshotPromises = buildAccessibleVisibleEventQueries({
1075  			db: options.db,
1076  			user: options.user,
1077  			userProfile: options.userProfile
1078  		}).map((query) => query.get());

1083  			db: options.db,
1084  			userId: options.user.uid
1085  		});
1086  		const [visibleSnapshotResults, ownSnapshot, inviteRecords] = await Promise.all([
1087  			Promise.allSettled(visibleSnapshotPromises),
1088  			ownSnapshotPromise,
1089  			inviteRecordsPromise
1090  		]);
1091  
1092  		const visibleSnapshots = visibleSnapshotResults.flatMap((result) => {
1093  			if (result.status === 'fulfilled') {
1094  				return [result.value];
1095  			}
1096  
1097  			console.warn('Dashboard visible events query failed:', result.reason);
1098  			return [];
1099  		});
1100  
1101  		const invitedEvents = await loadDirectInviteEvents({
1102  			db: options.db,
1103  			user: options.user,
1104  			userProfile: options.userProfile,
1105  			inviteRecords
1106  		});
1107  		const visibleEvents = buildUniqueEventList(
1108  			visibleSnapshots.flatMap((snapshot) => snapshot.docs.map((doc) => normalizeEventRecord({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() })))
1109  				.concat(invitedEvents)
1110  		).filter((event) => canUserAccessVisibleEvent(event, {
1111  			userId: options.user.uid,
1112  			userProfile: options.userProfile
1113  		}));
1114  		const ownEventMap = new Map();
1115  		const rawOwnEvents = [];
1116  
1117  		ownSnapshot.docs.forEach((doc) => {
1118  			const rawOwnEvent = { id: doc.id, ...doc.data() };
1119  			rawOwnEvents.push(rawOwnEvent);
1120  			ownEventMap.set(doc.id, normalizeEventRecord(rawOwnEvent));
1121  		});
1122  
1123  		const ownEvents = sortEventsByDateTime([...ownEventMap.values()]);
1124  		await backfillOwnAudienceScopeValues({
1125  			db: options.db,
1126  			ownEvents: rawOwnEvents
1127  		});
1128  		const joinedEventIds = await listJoinedEventIds({
1129  			db: options.db,
1130  			userId: options.user.uid,
1131  			events: buildUniqueEventList([...visibleEvents, ...ownEvents])
1132  		});
1133  		const joinedEvents = sortEventsByDateTime(visibleEvents.filter((event) => joinedEventIds.has(event.id)));
1134  		const calendarEvents = buildUniqueEventList([...ownEvents, ...joinedEvents, ...invitedEvents]);
1135  
1136  		return {
1137  			visibleEvents,
1138  			ownEvents,
1139  			joinedEventIds,
1140  			joinedEvents,
1141  			calendarEvents,
1142  			inviteRecords

firestore.rules:247-298canAccessEventDocument() speiler klientens synlighetsmodell
// firestore.rules:247-298
 247      // Allows reads for the event owner, booking reviewers, and residents who pass
 248      // the visible-event gate.
 249      // The rest of the file needs one shared definition of readable event access
 250      // instead of repeating these three paths.
 251      function canAccessEventDocument(data) {
 252        return isSignedIn()
 253          && (
 254          (data.keys().hasAny(['createdByUid']) && data.createdByUid == request.auth.uid)
 255          || hasBookingReviewAccess()
 256          || canAccessVisibleEvent(data)
 257          );
 258      }
 259  
 260      // Validates that an event write includes the full normalized audience fields in
 261      // a consistent combination.
 262      // The client and the rules both depend on these fields lining up, so writes are
 263      // forced into one predictable shape from the start.
 264      function isValidEventAudienceState(data) {
 265        return data.keys().hasAll(['audienceMode', 'audienceScopeValue', 'extraInviteUids', 'visibilityTargets', 'isPrivate'])
 266          && isValidAudienceMode(data.audienceMode)
 267          && data.audienceScopeValue is string
 268          && data.extraInviteUids is list
 269          && data.visibilityTargets is list
 270          && data.isPrivate is bool
 271          && data.isPrivate == (data.audienceMode == 'inviteOnly' && data.extraInviteUids.size() == 0)
 272          && (
 273          (data.audienceMode == 'anyone' && data.visibilityTargets.size() == 0 && data.audienceScopeValue == '')
 274          || (
 275            data.audienceMode == 'inviteOnly'
 276            && data.audienceScopeValue == ''
 277            && data.visibilityTargets.size() == data.extraInviteUids.size()
 278          )
 279          || (
 280            data.audienceMode == 'area'
 281            && data.visibilityTargets.size() > 0
 282            && data.audienceScopeValue == currentAreaScopeValue()
 283            && currentAreaTarget() == data.visibilityTargets[0]
 284          )
 285          || (
 286            data.audienceMode == 'block'
 287            && data.visibilityTargets.size() > 0
 288            && data.audienceScopeValue == currentBlockScopeValue()
 289            && currentBlockTarget() == data.visibilityTargets[0]
 290          )
 291          || (
 292            data.audienceMode == 'floor'
 293            && data.visibilityTargets.size() > 0
 294            && data.audienceScopeValue == currentFloorScopeValue()
 295            && currentFloorTarget() == data.visibilityTargets[0]
 296          )
 297          );
 298      }
firestore.rules:1100-1187events/{eventId} regler for les/opprett/oppdater/slett

1100      match /events/{eventId} {
1101        // Stores the main event documents shown in the calendar, profile views, and
1102        // event detail panels.

1104        // start in one normalized shape that later helpers can trust.
1105        // Creates new events only for the signed-in resident and only with the full
1106        // normalized audience fields already present.

1108        // edits, and invite syncing much simpler.

1136          && request.resource.data.description is string
1137          && request.resource.data.description.size() <= 2000
1138          && request.resource.data.eventDate is string
1139          && request.resource.data.startTime is string
1140          && request.resource.data.endTime is string

1154        // that need moderation visibility.
1155        // Event browsing is a shared feature, but private and pending records still
1156        // need one central gate instead of separate read rules everywhere.
1157        allow read: if canAccessEventDocument(resource.data);
1158  
1159        // Allows owner edits, owner cancellations, and reviewer approval decisions on
1160        // the narrow paths defined by the helpers above.
1161        // Event updates carry the most workflow risk in this file, so each allowed
1162        // branch is kept separate and tied to matching booking-request changes.

1185        // Delete access stays separate from normal edits so ownership remains the one
1186        // clear reason an event can disappear.

Hendelsesflyt

2. Brukeren åpner «opprett ny hendelse»

Det finnes to innganger for opprettelse: opprett-knappene i dashboard-kalenderen og det innebygde hendelsesoverlayet. Begge ender i samme skjemamodul og opprett-callback.

2.1 Dashboardet renderer opprett-valg

① Trigger: `renderDashboardCalendarPreview()` renderer tomme dagsplasser og legg til-knapper.
② Kjeden:
public/scripts/features/dashboard/dashboard-workspace.js:323-395render av forhåndsvisningskalender lager legg til-/opprett-knapper
// public/scripts/features/dashboard/dashboard-workspace.js:323-395
 323      // Fill the 7-day mini calendar on the dashboard home view.
 324      function renderCalendarEvents(events) {
 325        const dayBoxes = uiElements.previewCalendar?.dayBoxes || [];
 326  
 327        dayBoxes.forEach((box, index) => {
 328          const calendarEventsElement = box.querySelector('.calendar-events');
 329          const createButtonElement = box.querySelector('.dashboard-calendar-day-create-button-preview');
 330          const houseIconElement = box.querySelector('.dashboard-calendar-day-house-preview');
 331          const date = new Date();
 332          date.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
 333          date.setDate(date.getDate() + index);
 334          const eventDateKey = `${date.getFullYear()}-${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
 335          const isPastDate = isPastCalendarDate(eventDateKey);
 336      		const hasApprovedRoomBooking = hasApprovedCommonRoomBooking(events, eventDateKey);
 337  
 338          box.dataset.date = eventDateKey;
 339  
 340          const matchingItems = buildCalendarDayDisplayItems(
 341            events
 342              .filter((event) => event.eventDate === eventDateKey)
 343              .map(getCalendarEventDisplayItem)
 344          );
 345  
 346          box.classList.toggle('past', isPastDate);
 347          box.tabIndex = 0;
 348          box.setAttribute('role', 'button');
 349  
 350          calendarEventsElement.innerHTML = '';
 351  
 352          box.onclick = function (event) {
 353            if (event.target.closest('.dashboard-calendar-day-create-button')) {
 354              return;
 355            }
 356  
 357            handleCalendarDateActivation(eventDateKey, box);
 358          };
 359          box.onkeydown = function (event) {
 360            if (event.target.closest('.dashboard-calendar-day-create-button')) {
 361              return;
 362            }
 363  
 364            if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
 365              event.preventDefault();
 366              handleCalendarDateActivation(eventDateKey, box);
 367            }
 368          };
 369  
 370          if (createButtonElement) {
 371            createButtonElement.hidden = isPastDate;
 372            createButtonElement.onclick = function (event) {
 373              event.preventDefault();
 374              event.stopPropagation();
 375              openCreateEventForDate(eventDateKey);
 376            };
 377          }
 378  
 379          if (houseIconElement) {
 380  		  houseIconElement.hidden = isPastDate || hasApprovedRoomBooking;
 381          }
 382  
 383          if (matchingItems.length === 0) {
 384            const emptyItem = document.createElement('div');
 385            emptyItem.className = 'empty';
 386            emptyItem.textContent = 'No events...';
 387            calendarEventsElement.appendChild(emptyItem);
 388            return;
 389          }
 390  
 391          matchingItems.forEach((item) => {
 392            calendarEventsElement.appendChild(createCalendarDisplayChip('dashboard-preview-event', item));
 393          });
 394        });
 395      }

1879  				}

1882  			})

1885  			});

public/scripts/pages/dashboard-page.js:1819-1844initializeEmbeddedEventsOverlay()
// public/scripts/pages/dashboard-page.js:1819-1844
1819  	function initializeEmbeddedEventsOverlay() {
1820  		const embeddedEvents2Api = window.connectPages?.events2;
1821  		const bindEvents2Interactions = window.connectPages?.bindEvents2Interactions;
1822  
1823  		if (!embeddedEvents2Api || !bindEvents2Interactions) {
1824  			return null;
1825  		}
1826  
1827  		bindEvents2Interactions({
1828  			bindingKey: 'dashboard-events-overlay',
1829  			watchAuth: false,
1830  			onEventCreated: async (submitResult) => {
1831  				dashboardWorkspace.applyCreatedOwnEvent?.(submitResult);
1832  			},
1833  			onSubmitted: async () => {
1834  				// A successful create only needs a narrow dashboard refresh here. Rebuilding
1835  				// the full session can briefly reintroduce stale listener state and make the
1836  				// new event flash, jump, or disappear before the next snapshot settles.
1837  				if (currentAuthUser && currentProfile) {
1838  					await refreshDashboardWorkspace();
1839  				}
1840  			}
1841  		});
1842  
1843  		return embeddedEvents2Api;
1844  	}

public/scripts/pages/events-overlay-page.js:764-903bindEvents2Interactions() håndterer skjemaklikk og submit-kontroller
// public/scripts/pages/events-overlay-page.js:764-903
 764  	function bindEvents2Interactions(options = {}) {
 765  		const bindingKey = options.bindingKey || 'events2-default';
 766  		const bindingRegistry = window.connectPages.__events2Bindings || (window.connectPages.__events2Bindings = new Set());
 767  		if (options.onEventCreated !== undefined) {
 768  			eventsOverlayApi.setOnEventCreated(options.onEventCreated);
 769  		}
 770  		if (bindingRegistry.has(bindingKey)) {
 771  			return eventsOverlayApi;
 772  		}
 773  
 774  		bindingRegistry.add(bindingKey);
 775  
 776  		const visibilityCombobox = initVisibilityCombobox();
 777  
 778  		options.openTriggerElement?.addEventListener('click', async (event) => {
 779  			event.preventDefault();
 780  
 781  			try {
 782  				const sessionResult = await eventsOverlayApi.refreshSession(auth.currentUser);
 783  				if (!sessionResult?.user) {
 784  					return;
 785  				}
 786  
 787  				if (typeof eventsOverlayApi.refreshBusyDates === 'function') {
 788  					await eventsOverlayApi.refreshBusyDates();
 789  				}
 790  
 791  				visibilityCombobox.refreshProfile();
 792  				eventsOverlayApi.resetForm();
 793  				eventsOverlayApi.openSidebar();
 794  			} catch (error) {
 795  				eventsOverlayApi.showFeedback(error.message, true);
 796  			}

// public/scripts/features/events/events-form.js:101-126
 101  		// Validate the current create-event form values.
 102      function validate() {
 103        const nameEl = g('ev-name');
 104        const descEl = g('ev-desc');
 105        const termsEl = g('ev-terms');
 106        if (!nameEl || !descEl || !termsEl) {
 107          return false;
 108        }
 109        return eventFormUiApi.validateForm({
 110          values: {
 111            name: nameEl.value,
 112            description: descEl.value,
 113            location: g('ev-loc')?.value || '',
 114            requiresCustomLocation: state.place === 'loc',
 115            date: state.date,
 116            isPastDate: Boolean(state.date && typeof getTodayIso === 'function' && state.date < getTodayIso()),
 117            timeFrom: state.timeFrom,
 118            timeTo: state.timeTo,
 119            isCommonRoom: state.place === 'cr',
 120            isBusyDate: runtime.busyDates.has(state.date),
 121            acceptedTerms: termsEl.checked
 122          },
 123          elements: uiElements.eventFormValidation,
 124          toMinutes
 125        });
 126      }
public/scripts/ui-parts/event-form-ui.js:391-470validateFormUi() validerer feltdetaljer
// public/scripts/ui-parts/event-form-ui.js:391-470
 391  	// Validate the create/edit form values before a write is attempted.
 392  	// The validation order mirrors the user-facing form so the first visible errors
 393  	// still match the sections the user just interacted with.
 394  	function validateForm(options) {
 395  		let isValid = true;
 396  		const values = options.values;
 397  		const elements = options.elements;
 398  		const trimmedName = values.name.trim();
 399  
 400  		if (!trimmedName) {
 401  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 402  				errorElement: elements.nameErrorElement,
 403  				message: 'Event title is required.',
 404  				invalidElements: [elements.nameFieldElement]
 405  			}) && isValid;
 406  		} else if (trimmedName.length > MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH) {
 407  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 408  				errorElement: elements.nameErrorElement,
 409  				message: `Event title must be ${MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH} characters or fewer.`,
 410  				invalidElements: [elements.nameFieldElement]
 411  			}) && isValid;
 412  		}
 413  
 414  		if (!values.description.trim()) {
 415  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 416  				errorElement: elements.descriptionErrorElement,
 417  				message: 'Description is required.',
 418  				invalidElements: [elements.descriptionInputElement]
 419  			}) && isValid;
 420  		}
 421  
 422  		if (values.requiresCustomLocation && !values.location.trim()) {
 423  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 424  				errorElement: elements.locationErrorElement,
 425  				message: 'Location is required.',
 426  				applyInvalidState: function () {
 427  					setLocationFieldInvalidState(true);
 428  				}
 429  			}) && isValid;
 430  		}
 431  
 432  		if (!values.date) {
 433  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 434  				errorElement: elements.dateErrorElement,
 435  				message: 'Select a date.'
 436  			}) && isValid;
 437  		} else if (values.isPastDate) {
 438  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 439  				errorElement: elements.dateErrorElement,
 440  				message: 'Past dates cannot be selected.'
 441  			}) && isValid;
 442  		} else if (values.isCommonRoom && values.isBusyDate) {
 443  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 444  				errorElement: elements.dateErrorElement,
 445  				message: 'This day is already booked.'
 446  			}) && isValid;
 447  		}
 448  
 449  		if (!values.timeFrom || !values.timeTo) {
 450  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 451  				errorElement: elements.timeErrorElement,
 452  				message: 'Select a start and end time.'
 453  			}) && isValid;
 454  		} else if (options.toMinutes(values.timeTo) <= options.toMinutes(values.timeFrom)) {
 455  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 456  				errorElement: elements.timeErrorElement,
 457  				message: 'End time must be after start time.'
 458  			}) && isValid;
 459  		}
 460  
 461  		if (values.isCommonRoom && !values.acceptedTerms) {
 462  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 463  				errorElement: elements.termsErrorElement,
 464  				message: 'Accept the common-room terms to continue.',
 465  				invalidElements: [elements.termsCheckboxElement]
 466  			}) && isValid;
 467  		}
 468  
 469  		return isValid;
 470  	}
kaller validateForm() -> validateFormUi()
Hvorfor dette betyr noe: Validering hindrer ugyldig dato/tid/tittel/synlighet/romdata fra å bli Firestore-skrivinger.

Dataflyt

title, date, startTime, endTime, audience, og romfelt leses fra skjemakontroller.

valideringsfeil returneres til UI-et i stedet for å sende en API-forespørsel.

3.2 Sesjon og fellesromtilgjengelighet sjekkes

① Trigger: Gyldige skjemadata går inn i `createEventWithOptionalRoomBooking()` i skjemafeature-modulen.
② Kjeden:
public/scripts/features/events/events-form.js:128-178oppfrisk sesjon, sjekk opptatte romdatoer, privat solo-bekreftelse
// public/scripts/features/events/events-form.js:128-178
 128  		// Validate, refresh session state, create the event, and then drive the success UI.
 129      // Keeping the submit workflow here lets the page runtime stay focused on opening
 130      // and closing the overlay while this controller protects the form rules.
 131      async function handleSubmit() {
 132        if (!validate()) {
 133          eventFormUiApi.scrollToFirstVisibleError();
 134          return false;
 135        }
 136  
 137        const currentUser = auth.currentUser;
 138        let sessionResult = {
 139          user: currentUser,
 140          profile: runtime.currentProfile
 141        };
 142  
 143        if (typeof refreshSession === 'function') {
 144          sessionResult = await refreshSession(currentUser);
 145        }
 146  
 147        if (!sessionResult.user) {
 148          showFeedback('You must be logged in to create an event.', true);
 149          return false;
 150        }
 151  
 152        if (!sessionResult.profile) {
 153          showFeedback('Your profile is still loading. Please try again in a moment.', true);
 154          return false;
 155        }
 156  
 157        const user = sessionResult.user;
 158        const isCommonRoom = state.place === 'cr';
 159        const audienceMode = String(state.audienceMode || 'anyone');
 160        const extraInviteTargets = audienceMode === 'anyone' ? [] : (state.extraInviteTargets || []);
 161  
 162        if (isCommonRoom && typeof refreshBusyDates === 'function') {
 163          await refreshBusyDates();
 164          syncDateConflict();
 165  
 166          if (!validate()) {
 167            eventFormUiApi.scrollToFirstVisibleError();
 168            return false;
 169          }
 170        }
 171  
 172        if (audienceMode === 'inviteOnly' && extraInviteTargets.length === 0) {
 173          const keepPrivate = window.confirm('This will create a private solo event that only you can see. Continue?');
 174          if (!keepPrivate) {
 175            return false;
 176          }
 177        }
 178
public/scripts/features/events/events-core.js:179-233refreshSession() finner leilighets-/brukerkontekst
// public/scripts/features/events/events-core.js:179-233
 179  		// Refresh the current user/profile state from the shared session helpers.
 180      // The event overlay may open while the shared session store is still resolving,
 181      // so this helper prefers the shared session bridge before falling back to a
 182      // direct profile load.
 183      async function refreshSession(user) {
 184        const sharedSession = typeof getSessionState === 'function' ? getSessionState() : null;
 185  
 186        if (!user) {
 187          return applySharedSessionState({
 188            currentUser: null,
 189            currentProfile: null,
 190            isResolved: true,
 191            isLoadingProfile: false
 192          });
 193        }
 194  
 195        if (sharedSession?.currentUser?.uid === user.uid && sharedSession.isResolved && sharedSession.currentProfile) {
 196          return applySharedSessionState(sharedSession);
 197        }
 198  
 199        if (sharedSession?.currentUser?.uid === user.uid && !sharedSession.isResolved && typeof waitForSessionResolution === 'function') {
 200          const resolvedSession = await waitForSessionResolution();
 201          if (resolvedSession?.currentUser?.uid === user.uid) {
 202            return applySharedSessionState(resolvedSession);
 203          }
 204        }
 205  
 206        if (typeof refreshSharedSession === 'function') {
 207          const refreshedSession = await refreshSharedSession();
 208          if (refreshedSession?.currentUser?.uid === user.uid) {
 209            return applySharedSessionState(refreshedSession);
 210          }
 211        }
 212  
 213        const signedInUser = requireSignedInUser(user);
 214  
 215        if (!signedInUser) {
 216          return applySharedSessionState({
 217            currentUser: null,
 218            currentProfile: null,
 219            isResolved: true,
 220            isLoadingProfile: false
 221          });
 222        }
 223  
 224        runtime.currentProfile = await ensureUserProfile(signedInUser);
 225        runtime.currentUser = signedInUser;
 226        runtime.isSessionResolved = true;
 227        runtime.isSessionLoading = false;
 228  
 229        return {
 230          user: signedInUser,
 231          profile: runtime.currentProfile
 232        };
 233      }
public/scripts/features/events/events-core.js:235-313opptatt-dato-lytter/fallback-spørring
// public/scripts/features/events/events-core.js:235-313
 235  		// Fallback path for loading already-approved common-room dates when the live
 236  		// listener is unavailable.
 237      // The create form still needs blocked-date feedback even when the preferred
 238      // live listener cannot be used, so this path rebuilds the busy-date set from
 239      // readable event data.
 240      async function loadApprovedBookingDatesFallback(onChanged) {
 241        const activeUser = runtime.currentUser || auth.currentUser || null;
 242        const activeProfile = runtime.currentProfile || {};
 243        let visibleEvents = [];
 244  
 245        if (activeUser && typeof eventDataApi.buildAccessibleVisibleEventQueries === 'function') {
 246          const visibleQueryResults = await Promise.allSettled(
 247            eventDataApi.buildAccessibleVisibleEventQueries({
 248              db,
 249              user: activeUser,
 250              userProfile: activeProfile
 251            }).map((query) => query.get())
 252          );
 253  
 254          visibleEvents = eventDataApi.buildUniqueEventList(
 255            visibleQueryResults.flatMap((result) => {
 256              if (result.status !== 'fulfilled') {
 257                return [];
 258              }
 259  
 260              return result.value.docs.map((doc) => eventDataApi.normalizeEventRecord({
 261                id: doc.id,
 262                ...doc.data()
 263              }));
 264            })
 265          );
 266        }
 267  
 268        if (!visibleEvents.length) {
 269          visibleEvents = await eventDataApi.listVisibleEvents({ db });
 270        }
 271  
 272        const approvedCommonRoomDates = visibleEvents
 273          .filter((event) => event.useCommonRoom && event.eventDate)
 274          .map((event) => event.eventDate);
 275  
 276        runtime.busyDates = new Set(approvedCommonRoomDates);
 277        if (typeof onChanged === 'function') {
 278          onChanged(runtime.busyDates);
 279        }
 280      }
 281  
 282  		// Refresh the set of blocked common-room dates once.
 283      async function refreshBusyDates(onChanged) {
 284        await loadApprovedBookingDatesFallback(onChanged);
 285        return runtime.busyDates;
 286      }
 287  
 288  		// Open the live approved-booking-date listener and fall back gracefully if it fails.
 289      function listenForApprovedBookings(onChanged) {
 290        if (runtime.stopApprovedBookingDatesListener) {
 291          runtime.stopApprovedBookingDatesListener();
 292        }
 293  
 294        refreshBusyDates(onChanged).catch((error) => {
 295          console.warn('refreshBusyDates:', error);
 296        });
 297  
 298        runtime.stopApprovedBookingDatesListener = roomDataApi.subscribeApprovedBookingDates({
 299          db,
 300          onChange(approvedDates) {
 301            runtime.busyDates = new Set(approvedDates);
 302            if (typeof onChanged === 'function') {
 303              onChanged(runtime.busyDates);
 304            }
 305          },
 306          onError(error) {
 307            console.warn('listenForApprovedBookings:', error);
 308            loadApprovedBookingDatesFallback(onChanged).catch((fallbackError) => {
 309              console.error('loadApprovedBookingDatesFallback:', fallbackError);
 310            });
 311          }
 312        });
 313      }
sjekker sesjon og opptatte datoer for fellesrom
Hvorfor dette betyr noe: Opprettelse av hendelser avhenger av leilighetsmedlemskap og romtilgjengelighet; å hoppe over dette ville tillatt usynlige eller konfliktende hendelser.

Dataflyt

session.user, apartmentId og homeId flyter inn i opprett-payloaden.

busyCommonRoomDates blokkerer eller advarer om romkonflikter.

public/scripts/data/events-data.js:1-274målgruppehjelpere normaliserer synlighet/deltakere
// public/scripts/data/events-data.js:1-274
   1  // Author: Amir, Sondre
   2  // Shared event data layer.
   3  //
   4  // This file owns the Firestore reads, writes, normalization helpers, and invite
   5  // synchronization used by the event system.
   6  // Keeping those rules together matters because events, attendees, booking
   7  // requests, and invite records all have to keep the same event shape in sync.
   8  (function initializeConnectEventData() {
   9  	// Audience modes used by the newer visibility model.
  10  	// Keeping the allowed modes in one list prevents the UI and data layer from
  11  	// drifting into slightly different visibility vocabularies.
  12  	const AUDIENCE_MODES = ['anyone', 'area', 'block', 'floor', 'inviteOnly'];
  13  	const INVITE_RESPONSE_PENDING = 'pending';
  14  	const INVITE_RESPONSE_ACCEPTED = 'accepted';
  15  	// Reuse the shared event-title limit so create, edit, and invite-sync paths all
  16  	// talk about the same title rule.
  17  	const MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH = Number(window.connectFeatures?.events?.constants?.MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH) || 25;
  18  
  19  	// Normalize any input into a trimmed string.
  20  	// Small string cleanup helpers like this keep later comparisons stable when data
  21  	// may come from form fields, Firestore docs, or older saved records.
  22  	function sanitizeString(value) {
  23  		return String(value || '').trim();
  24  	}
  25  
  26  	// New and renamed events must stay within the current title limit, but older
  27  	// saved titles are still allowed to remain unchanged during later edits.
  28  	function validateEventTitle(value, options = {}) {
  29  		const normalizedTitle = sanitizeString(value);
  30  		const previousTitle = sanitizeString(options.previousTitle);
  31  		const allowUnchangedLegacyTitle = Boolean(options.allowUnchangedLegacyTitle)
  32  			&& previousTitle.length > MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH
  33  			&& normalizedTitle === previousTitle;
  34  
  35  		if (!normalizedTitle) {
  36  			throw new Error('Event title is required.');
  37  		}
  38  
  39  		if (normalizedTitle.length > MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH && !allowUnchangedLegacyTitle) {
  40  			throw new Error(`Event title must be ${MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH} characters or fewer.`);
  41  		}
  42  
  43  		return normalizedTitle;
  44  	}
  45  
  46  	// Return unique non-empty strings in their first-seen order.
  47  	function uniqueStrings(values) {
  48  		const seen = new Set();
  49  		return (Array.isArray(values) ? values : []).reduce((result, value) => {
  50  			const trimmed = sanitizeString(value);
  51  			if (trimmed && !seen.has(trimmed)) {
  52  				seen.add(trimmed);
  53  				result.push(trimmed);
  54  			}
  55  			return result;
  56  		}, []);
  57  	}
  58  
  59  	// Clamp audience mode values to the supported set.
  60  	// Invalid or legacy values collapse to an empty string here so later helpers can
  61  	// decide on one safe fallback instead of each doing its own mode guessing.
  62  	function sanitizeAudienceMode(value) {
  63  		return AUDIENCE_MODES.includes(value) ? value : '';
  64  	}
  65  
  66  	// Normalize one invited-resident target into the stored event visibility shape.
  67  	// Event docs and invite pickers pass around slightly different resident objects,
  68  	// so this helper converts them into the one stored target shape the rest of the
  69  	// event system expects.
  70  	function sanitizeResidentInviteTarget(target) {
  71  		if (!target || typeof target !== 'object') {
  72  			return null;
  73  		}
  74  
  75  		const uid = sanitizeString(target.value || target.uid);
  76  		if (!uid) {
  77  			return null;
  78  		}
  79  
  80  		return {
  81  			type: 'resident',
  82  			value: uid,
  83  			label: sanitizeString(target.label || target.displayName || 'Invited resident')
  84  		};
  85  	}
  86  
  87  	// Deduplicate and normalize the array of invited residents.
  88  	function normalizeResidentInviteTargets(targets) {
  89  		const deduped = new Map();
  90  
  91  		(targets || []).forEach((target) => {
  92  			const residentTarget = sanitizeResidentInviteTarget(target);
  93  			if (residentTarget && !deduped.has(residentTarget.value)) {
  94  				deduped.set(residentTarget.value, residentTarget);
  95  			}
  96  		});
  97  
  98  		return [...deduped.values()];
  99  	}
 100  
 101  	// Build one resident-target object from a user/profile-like record.
 102  	function buildResidentInviteTarget(person) {
 103  		return sanitizeResidentInviteTarget({
 104  			value: person?.uid || person?.value,
 105  			label: person?.displayName || person?.label
 106  		});
 107  	}
 108  
 109  	// Build the area/block/floor scope target based on the creator's housing profile.
 110  	function buildAudienceScopeTarget(audienceMode, profile) {
 111  		const area = sanitizeString(profile?.housingArea);
 112  		const block = sanitizeString(profile?.housingBlock);
 113  		const floor = sanitizeString(profile?.floor);
 114  
 115  		if (audienceMode === 'area' && area) {
 116  			return {
 117  				type: 'area',
 118  				value: area,
 119  				label: `Housing area: ${area}`,
 120  				housingArea: area
 121  			};
 122  		}
 123  
 124  		if (audienceMode === 'block' && area && block) {
 125  			return {
 126  				type: 'block',
 127  				value: `${area}:${block}`,
 128  				label: `Housing block: ${block}`,
 129  				housingArea: area,
 130  				housingBlock: block
 131  			};
 132  		}
 133  
 134  		if (audienceMode === 'floor' && area && block && floor) {
 135  			return {
 136  				type: 'floor',
 137  				value: `${area}:${block}:${floor}`,
 138  				label: `Floor: ${floor}`,
 139  				housingArea: area,
 140  				housingBlock: block,
 141  				floor
 142  			};
 143  		}
 144  
 145  		return null;
 146  	}
 147  
 148  	// Older event docs may only have legacy visibility fields, so infer the intended
 149  	// audience mode from those older shapes.
 150  	// This lets the current UI and data layer keep working with older documents while
 151  	// gradually nudging them toward the newer normalized audience model.
 152  	function pickLegacyAudienceMode(event, visibilityTargets) {
 153  		const explicitAudienceMode = sanitizeAudienceMode(event?.audienceMode);
 154  		if (explicitAudienceMode) {
 155  			return explicitAudienceMode;
 156  		}
 157  
 158  		if (Boolean(event?.isPrivate)) {
 159  			return 'inviteOnly';
 160  		}
 161  
 162  		const floorTarget = visibilityTargets.find((target) => target?.type === 'floor');
 163  		if (floorTarget) {
 164  			return 'floor';
 165  		}
 166  
 167  		const blockTarget = visibilityTargets.find((target) => target?.type === 'block');
 168  		if (blockTarget) {
 169  			return 'block';
 170  		}
 171  
 172  		const areaTarget = visibilityTargets.find((target) => target?.type === 'area');
 173  		if (areaTarget) {
 174  			return 'area';
 175  		}
 176  
 177  		if (visibilityTargets.some((target) => target?.type === 'resident')) {
 178  			return 'inviteOnly';
 179  		}
 180  
 181  		return 'anyone';
 182  	}
 183  
 184  	// Keep only well-formed visibility target objects.
 185  	function normalizeVisibilityTargets(value) {
 186  		return Array.isArray(value)
 187  			? value.filter((target) => target && typeof target === 'object' && sanitizeString(target.type) && sanitizeString(target.value))
 188  			: [];
 189  	}
 190  
 191  	// Recover the stored scope value for area/block/floor events, even on older docs.
 192  	function normalizeAudienceScopeValue(event, audienceMode, visibilityTargets) {
 193  		if (!['area', 'block', 'floor'].includes(audienceMode)) {
 194  			return '';
 195  		}
 196  
 197  		const explicitScopeValue = sanitizeString(event?.audienceScopeValue);
 198  		if (explicitScopeValue) {
 199  			return explicitScopeValue;
 200  		}
 201  
 202  		const scopeTarget = visibilityTargets.find((target) => target?.type === audienceMode);
 203  		return sanitizeString(scopeTarget?.value);
 204  	}
 205  
 206  	// Return the fully normalized audience information for any event record.
 207  	// Normalizing once in one shared helper keeps later reads, UI rendering, and
 208  	// update logic from repeating the same legacy-compatibility work.
 209  	function normalizeEventAudience(event) {
 210  		const visibilityTargets = normalizeVisibilityTargets(event?.visibilityTargets);
 211  		const extraInviteTargets = normalizeResidentInviteTargets(
 212  			visibilityTargets.filter((target) => target.type === 'resident')
 213  		);
 214  		const extraInviteUids = uniqueStrings([
 215  			...(Array.isArray(event?.extraInviteUids) ? event.extraInviteUids : []),
 216  			...extraInviteTargets.map((target) => target.value)
 217  		]);
 218  		const audienceMode = pickLegacyAudienceMode(event, visibilityTargets);
 219  		const audienceScopeValue = normalizeAudienceScopeValue(event, audienceMode, visibilityTargets);
 220  		const isPrivate = audienceMode === 'inviteOnly' && extraInviteUids.length === 0;
 221  
 222  		return {
 223  			audienceMode,
 224  			audienceScopeValue,
 225  			extraInviteUids,
 226  			extraInviteTargets,
 227  			visibilityTargets,
 228  			isPrivate
 229  		};
 230  	}
 231  
 232  	// Return an event record with all newer normalized audience fields added.
 233  	function normalizeEventRecord(event) {
 234  		if (!event || typeof event !== 'object') {
 235  			return event;
 236  		}
 237  
 238  		return {
 239  			...event,
 240  			...normalizeEventAudience(event)
 241  		};
 242  	}
 243  
 244  	// Build the stored audience fields for a new or updated event from form input.
 245  	// New writes should leave this helper already in the normalized event shape so
 246  	// later reads do not need to guess what the user intended.
 247  	function buildAudienceFields(options) {
 248  		const audienceMode = sanitizeAudienceMode(options?.audienceMode) || 'anyone';
 249  		const actorProfile = options?.actorProfile || {};
 250  		const extraInviteTargets = audienceMode === 'anyone'
 251  			? []
 252  			: normalizeResidentInviteTargets(options?.extraInviteTargets || []);
 253  		const baseTarget = buildAudienceScopeTarget(audienceMode, actorProfile);
 254  
 255  		if ((audienceMode === 'area' || audienceMode === 'block' || audienceMode === 'floor') && !baseTarget) {
 256  			throw new Error('Your housing profile is incomplete. Refresh the page and try again.');
 257  		}
 258  
 259  		const visibilityTargets = [
 260  			...(baseTarget ? [baseTarget] : []),
 261  			...extraInviteTargets
 262  		];
 263  		const extraInviteUids = uniqueStrings(extraInviteTargets.map((target) => target.value));
 264  		const audienceScopeValue = sanitizeString(baseTarget?.value);
 265  		const isPrivate = audienceMode === 'inviteOnly' && extraInviteUids.length === 0;
 266  
 267  		return {
 268  			audienceMode,
 269  			audienceScopeValue,
 270  			extraInviteUids,
 271  			visibilityTargets,
 272  			isPrivate
 273  		};
 274  	}

// public/scripts/data/events-data.js:568-726
 568  	// Build or refresh the stored invite document payload for one invited resident.
 569  	function buildEventInviteRecordData(options) {
 570  		const normalizedEvent = normalizeEventRecord(options.eventData || {});
 571  		const inviteTarget = sanitizeResidentInviteTarget(options.inviteTarget);
 572  		const existingInvite = normalizeEventInviteRecord(options.existingInvite || {});
 573  		const responseStatus = existingInvite?.responseStatus === INVITE_RESPONSE_ACCEPTED
 574  			? INVITE_RESPONSE_ACCEPTED
 575  			: INVITE_RESPONSE_PENDING;
 576  
 577  		if (!inviteTarget) {
 578  			return null;
 579  		}
 580  
 581  		return {
 582  			eventId: sanitizeString(options.eventId),
 583  			invitedUid: inviteTarget.value,
 584  			invitedName: inviteTarget.label,
 585  			createdByUid: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.createdByUid),
 586  			createdByName: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.createdByName || 'Resident'),
 587  			eventName: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.name || 'Untitled event'),
 588  			eventDate: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.eventDate),
 589  			startTime: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.startTime),
 590  			endTime: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.endTime),
 591  			eventStatus: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.status || 'visible'),
 592  			audienceMode: sanitizeAudienceMode(normalizedEvent.audienceMode) || 'inviteOnly',
 593  			responseStatus,
 594  			createdAt: existingInvite?.createdAt || options.serverTimestamp(),
 595  			updatedAt: options.serverTimestamp(),
 596  			respondedAt: responseStatus === INVITE_RESPONSE_ACCEPTED
 597  				? (existingInvite?.respondedAt || null)
 598  				: null
 599  		};
 600  	}

 640  					.doc(buildEventInviteDocId(eventId, inviteTarget.value))

 652  				return null;
 653  			}

 667  					...doc.data()
 668  				}));

 680  	// Make the event invite documents match the event's current extra-invite targets.
 681  	async function syncEventInviteDocuments(options) {
 682  		const inviteTargets = getResidentInviteTargetsForEvent(options.eventData || {});
 683  		const existingInvites = Array.isArray(options.existingInvites)
 684  			? options.existingInvites.map((invite) => normalizeEventInviteRecord(invite))
 685  			: await listEventInviteRecordsForEvent({
 686  				db: options.db,
 687  				eventId: options.eventId
 688  			});
 689  		const existingByUid = new Map();
 690  		const batch = options.batch || options.db.batch();
 691  
 692  		existingInvites.forEach((invite) => {
 693  			existingByUid.set(invite.invitedUid, invite);
 694  		});
 695  
 696  		const nextInvitedUids = new Set(inviteTargets.map((target) => target.value));
 697  
 698  		existingInvites.forEach((invite) => {
 699  			if (!nextInvitedUids.has(invite.invitedUid) && invite.ref) {
 700  				batch.delete(invite.ref);
 701  			}
 702  		});
 703  
 704  		inviteTargets.forEach((inviteTarget) => {
 705  			const inviteData = buildEventInviteRecordData({
 706  				eventId: options.eventId,
 707  				eventData: options.eventData,
 708  				inviteTarget,
 709  				existingInvite: existingByUid.get(inviteTarget.value),
 710  				serverTimestamp: options.serverTimestamp
 711  			});
 712  
 713  			if (!inviteData) {
 714  				return;
 715  			}
 716  
 717  			batch.set(
 718  				getEventInvitesCollection(options.db).doc(buildEventInviteDocId(options.eventId, inviteTarget.value)),
 719  				inviteData
 720  			);
 721  		});
 722  
 723  		if (!options.batch) {
 724  			await batch.commit();
 725  		}
 726  	}

1146  	// Create a new event, and create a linked booking request too when the event uses
1147  	// the common room.
1148  	async function createEventWithOptionalRoomBooking(options) {
1149  		const db = options.db;
1150  		const formValues = options.formValues;
1151  		const actor = options.actor;
1152  		const serverTimestamp = options.serverTimestamp;
1153  		const useCommonRoom = Boolean(formValues.useCommonRoom);
1154  		const eventTitle = validateEventTitle(formValues.name);
1155  		const trimmedLocation = String(formValues.location || '').trim();
1156  		const audienceFields = buildAudienceFields({
1157  			audienceMode: formValues.audienceMode,
1158  			actorProfile: options.actorProfile,
1159  			extraInviteTargets: formValues.extraInviteTargets
1160  		});
1161  		const eventRef = db.collection('events').doc();
1162  		const eventData = {
1163  			name: eventTitle,
1164  			description: String(formValues.description || '').trim(),
1165  			eventDate: formValues.eventDate,
1166  			startTime: formValues.startTime,
1167  			endTime: formValues.endTime || '',
1168  			useCommonRoom,
1169  			location: useCommonRoom ? (window.COMMON_ROOM_CONFIG?.name || 'Common Room') : (trimmedLocation || null),
1170  			isPrivate: audienceFields.isPrivate,
1171  			audienceMode: audienceFields.audienceMode,
1172  			audienceScopeValue: audienceFields.audienceScopeValue,
1173  			extraInviteUids: audienceFields.extraInviteUids,
1174  			visibilityTargets: audienceFields.visibilityTargets,
1175  			status: useCommonRoom ? 'pending' : 'visible',
1176  			createdByUid: actor.uid,
1177  			createdByName: actor.displayName,
1178  			createdAt: serverTimestamp()
1179  		};
1180  		const batch = db.batch();
1181  
1182  		if (useCommonRoom) {
1183  			const bookingRequestRef = db.collection('bookingRequests').doc();
1184  			eventData.bookingRequestId = bookingRequestRef.id;
1185  			batch.set(bookingRequestRef, {
1186  				eventId: eventRef.id,
1187  				eventName: eventData.name,
1188  				eventDate: eventData.eventDate,
1189  				startTime: eventData.startTime,
1190  				endTime: eventData.endTime || '',
1191  				isPrivate: eventData.isPrivate,
1192  				requestedByUid: actor.uid,
1193  				requesterName: actor.displayName,
1194  				status: 'pending',
1195  				createdAt: serverTimestamp()
1196  			});
1197  		}
1198  
1199  		batch.set(eventRef, eventData);
1200  		await syncEventInviteDocuments({
1201  			db,
1202  			batch,
1203  			eventId: eventRef.id,
1204  			eventData,
1205  			existingInvites: [],
1206  			serverTimestamp
1207  		});
1208  
1209  		// Event creators should always count as members of their own event, including
1210  		// private solo and invite-only events, so calendar/chat/profile state stays
1211  		// consistent without requiring a second join flow.
1212  		batch.set(eventRef.collection('attendees').doc(actor.uid), {
1213  			uid: actor.uid,
1214  			displayName: actor.displayName,
1215  			joinedAt: serverTimestamp()
1216  		});
1217  
1218  		await batch.commit();
1219  
1220  		return {
1221  			eventId: eventRef.id,
1222  			event: eventData
1223  		};
1224  	}
skriver Firestore-sti events/{eventId}
Hvorfor dette betyr noe: Hendelsesdokumentet er den kanoniske posten som detalj-, redigerings-, avlysnings- og sletteflytene senere leser.

Dataflyt

request-payload: tittel, dato, tider, beskrivelse, målgruppe, inviterte bruker-id-er, apartmentId, eier-uid og rombestillingsdata.

respons: opprettet hendelses-id pluss eventuell bestillingsrequest-id/status som brukes av oppfølgings-UI og dashboard-tilstand.

firestore.rules:260-298isValidEventAudienceState()
// firestore.rules:260-298
 260      // Validates that an event write includes the full normalized audience fields in
 261      // a consistent combination.
 262      // The client and the rules both depend on these fields lining up, so writes are
 263      // forced into one predictable shape from the start.
 264      function isValidEventAudienceState(data) {
 265        return data.keys().hasAll(['audienceMode', 'audienceScopeValue', 'extraInviteUids', 'visibilityTargets', 'isPrivate'])
 266          && isValidAudienceMode(data.audienceMode)
 267          && data.audienceScopeValue is string
 268          && data.extraInviteUids is list
 269          && data.visibilityTargets is list
 270          && data.isPrivate is bool
 271          && data.isPrivate == (data.audienceMode == 'inviteOnly' && data.extraInviteUids.size() == 0)
 272          && (
 273          (data.audienceMode == 'anyone' && data.visibilityTargets.size() == 0 && data.audienceScopeValue == '')
 274          || (
 275            data.audienceMode == 'inviteOnly'
 276            && data.audienceScopeValue == ''
 277            && data.visibilityTargets.size() == data.extraInviteUids.size()
 278          )
 279          || (
 280            data.audienceMode == 'area'
 281            && data.visibilityTargets.size() > 0
 282            && data.audienceScopeValue == currentAreaScopeValue()
 283            && currentAreaTarget() == data.visibilityTargets[0]
 284          )
 285          || (
 286            data.audienceMode == 'block'
 287            && data.visibilityTargets.size() > 0
 288            && data.audienceScopeValue == currentBlockScopeValue()
 289            && currentBlockTarget() == data.visibilityTargets[0]
 290          )
 291          || (
 292            data.audienceMode == 'floor'
 293            && data.visibilityTargets.size() > 0
 294            && data.audienceScopeValue == currentFloorScopeValue()
 295            && currentFloorTarget() == data.visibilityTargets[0]
 296          )
 297          );
 298      }

firestore.rules:1238-1266regler for eventInvites/{inviteId}
// firestore.rules:1238-1266
1238    match /eventInvites/{inviteId} {
1239      // Stores direct event-invite documents for invite notifications and invite
1240      // acceptance flows.
1241      // Keeping invites separate from the main event document makes it much easier
1242      // to load a resident's invitations without scanning whole events.
1243      // Allows reads for the invitee, the event creator, and booking reviewers.
1244      // Those are the only roles that need to inspect one invite document directly.
1245      allow read: if isSignedIn()
1246        && (
1247          resource.data.invitedUid == request.auth.uid
1248          || resource.data.createdByUid == request.auth.uid
1249          || hasBookingReviewAccess()
1250        );
1251      // Allows invite creation only through the validated creator helper.
1252      // Invite ids and payloads are derived from event data, so the write path stays
1253      // narrow and predictable.
1254      allow create: if creatorCanCreateEventInvite(inviteId);
1255      // Allows only the specific update paths defined for creators, invitees, and
1256      // booking reviewers.
1257      // Invite documents repeat event data, so updates are restricted tightly to keep
1258      // copied metadata and response state from drifting.
1259      allow update: if creatorCanUpdateEventInvite(inviteId)
1260        || invitedResidentCanAcceptEventInvite()
1261        || reviewerCanUpdateEventInviteStatus();
1262      // Allows deletion only when the creator helper confirms the invite is no longer
1263      // backed by the event.
1264      // Invites are derived records, so delete access follows the lifecycle of the
1265      // source event and its invite list.
1266      allow delete: if creatorCanDeleteEventInvite();

   2  // Booking-request list view.
   3  //
   4  // This feature handles the card rendering used on the booking-requests page,
   5  // including request counts and the confirm dialog used before approve/deny.
   6  // The page layer can hand this feature fresh request lists, while this file keeps
   7  // the grouped-card presentation and timeline-specific display rules in one place.
   8  (function initializeBookingRequestsViewFeature() {
   9    // Build the resident-facing booking-request list renderer from the current page
  10    // state and cached DOM references.
  11    function createBookingRequestsView(options) {
  12      const state = options.state;
  13      const ui = options.ui;
  14      const canReviewBookingRequest = options.canReviewBookingRequest;
  15      const getTodayIso = options.getTodayIso;
  16      const TIMELINE_PENDING = 'pending';
  17      const TIMELINE_HISTORY = 'history';
  18      const DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  19  
  20      if (state.activeTimeline !== TIMELINE_HISTORY) {
  21        state.activeTimeline = TIMELINE_PENDING;
  22      }
  23  
  24      // Escape text before building the request cards from HTML strings.
  25      function escapeHtml(text) {
  26        var div = document.createElement('div');
  27        div.textContent = text;
  28        return div.innerHTML;
  29      }
  30  
  31      // Convert a stored ISO date string into a Date object anchored at midday so
  32      // timezone shifts do not move the request onto the wrong local day.
  33      function parseDateIso(dateIso) {
  34        if (!dateIso || typeof dateIso !== 'string') {
  35          return null;
  36        }
  37  
  38        var parsedDate = new Date(dateIso + 'T12:00:00');
  39        return Number.isNaN(parsedDate.getTime()) ? null : parsedDate;
  40      }
  41  
  42      // Normalize a date to the Monday of that week so pending requests can be grouped
  43      // by "This week", "Next week", and so on.
  44      function startOfWeek(date) {
  45        var normalizedDate = new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate());
  46        var weekday = normalizedDate.getDay();
  47        var mondayOffset = weekday === 0 ? -6 : 1 - weekday;
  48        normalizedDate.setDate(normalizedDate.getDate() + mondayOffset);
  49        normalizedDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  50        return normalizedDate;
  51      }
  52  
  53      // Return either the active pending view or the derived history view depending on
  54      // which timeline tab is selected.
  55      function getTimelineRequests() {
  56        if (state.activeTimeline === TIMELINE_HISTORY) {
  57          return getHistoryTimelineRequests();
  58        }
  59  
  60        return (state.pendingRequests || []).slice();
  61      }
  62  
  63      // History includes both already-reviewed requests and pending requests whose
  64      // event date is already in the past.
  65      function getHistoryTimelineRequests() {
  66        var todayIso = getTodayIso();
  67        var pastPendingRequests = (state.pendingRequests || []).filter(function (request) {
  68          return request.status === 'pending' && request.eventDate && request.eventDate < todayIso;
  69        });
  70  
  71        return (state.historyRequests || []).concat(pastPendingRequests);
  72      }
  73  
  74      // Normalize Firestore timestamps and older plain values into one millisecond
  75      // number so same-day conflict groups can prioritize the oldest submission.
  76      function getRequestCreatedAtMs(request) {
  77        var createdAt = request && request.createdAt;
  78  
  79        if (!createdAt) {
  80          return 0;
  81        }
  82  
  83        if (typeof createdAt.toDate === 'function') {
  84          return createdAt.toDate().getTime();
  85        }
  86  
  87        if (typeof createdAt.seconds === 'number') {
  88          return createdAt.seconds * 1000;
  89        }
  90  
  91        var parsedDate = new Date(createdAt);
  92        return Number.isNaN(parsedDate.getTime()) ? 0 : parsedDate.getTime();
  93      }
  94  
  95      // Pending requests should stay in chronological day order. When multiple
  96      // requests target the same day, the oldest submitted request comes first.
  97      function comparePendingRequests(first, second) {
  98        var dateComparison = String(first.eventDate || '').localeCompare(String(second.eventDate || ''));
  99        if (dateComparison !== 0) {
 100          return dateComparison;
 101        }
 102  
 103        var createdAtComparison = getRequestCreatedAtMs(first) - getRequestCreatedAtMs(second);
 104        if (createdAtComparison !== 0) {
 105          return createdAtComparison;
 106        }
 107  
 108        var startTimeComparison = String(first.startTime || '').localeCompare(String(second.startTime || ''));
 109        if (startTimeComparison !== 0) {
 110          return startTimeComparison;
 111        }
 112  
 113        return String(first.id || '').localeCompare(String(second.id || ''));
 114      }
 115  
 116      // History is ordered newest day first so recently handled items stay near the top.
 117      function compareHistoryRequests(first, second) {
 118        var dateComparison = String(second.eventDate || '').localeCompare(String(first.eventDate || ''));
 119        if (dateComparison !== 0) {
 120          return dateComparison;
 121        }
 122  
 123        var timeComparison = String(second.startTime || '').localeCompare(String(first.startTime || ''));
 124        if (timeComparison !== 0) {
 125          return timeComparison;
 126        }
 127  
 128        return String(second.id || '').localeCompare(String(first.id || ''));
 129      }
 130  
 131      // Format the long readable date label used in the grouped pending sections.
 132      function formatDayLabel(dateIso) {
 133        var parsedDate = parseDateIso(dateIso);
 134  
 135        if (!parsedDate) {
 136          return 'Unknown date';
 137        }
 138  
 139        return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
 140          weekday: 'long',
 141          day: 'numeric',
 142          month: 'long',
 143          year: 'numeric'
 144        }).format(parsedDate);
 145      }
 146  
 147      // Build the coarse week bucket label used above pending request groups.
 148      function formatWeekLabel(dateIso) {
 149        var parsedDate = parseDateIso(dateIso);
 150        var today = parseDateIso(getTodayIso());
 151  
 152        if (!parsedDate || !today) {
 153          return 'Upcoming weeks';
 154        }
 155  
 156        var weekOffset = Math.round((startOfWeek(parsedDate).getTime() - startOfWeek(today).getTime()) / (7 * DAY_MS));
 157  
 158        if (weekOffset <= 0) {
 159          return 'This week';
 160        }
 161  
 162        if (weekOffset === 1) {
 163          return 'Next week';
 164        }
 165  
 166        return weekOffset + ' weeks from now';
 167      }
 168  
 169      // Group pending requests first by date and then by week so conflicting requests
 170      // for the same day are shown together.
 171  		// The grouping order matters because reviewers need to spot same-day conflicts
 172  		// before reading individual request details.
 173      function buildPendingSections() {
 174        var pendingRequests = getTimelineRequests().sort(comparePendingRequests);
 175        var groupsByDate = {};
 176        var orderedDates = [];
 177  
 178        pendingRequests.forEach(function (request) {
 179          var dateKey = request.eventDate || 'unknown';
 180
public/scripts/data/rooms-data.js:96-188reviewBookingRequestDecision() oppdaterer bestilling og koblet hendelse
// public/scripts/data/rooms-data.js:96-188
  96  	async function reviewBookingRequestDecision(options) {
  97  		const request = options.request;
  98  		const decision = options.decision;
  99  		const actorDisplayName = options.actor.displayName || 'Approver';
 100  		const siblingSnapshot = request.eventDate
 101  			? await options.db.collection('bookingRequests').where('eventDate', '==', request.eventDate).get()
 102  			: null;
 103  		const siblingRequests = siblingSnapshot
 104  			? siblingSnapshot.docs.map((doc) => ({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }))
 105  			: [request];
 106  
 107  		if (decision === 'approved') {
 108  			const approvedSibling = siblingRequests.find((entry) => entry.id !== request.id && entry.status === 'approved');
 109  
 110  			if (approvedSibling) {
 111  				throw new Error('Another booking request for ' + (request.eventDate || 'this day') + ' has already been approved.');
 112  			}
 113  		}
 114  
 115  		const updatedRequests = [];
 116  
 117  		// Apply one approval or denial to a single booking request plus its linked event.
 118  		// This helper is reused for the main decision and for same-day auto-denials so
 119  		// both paths keep the same event, invite, and attendee side effects.
 120  		async function applyDecision(targetRequest, nextStatus) {
 121  			const nextEventStatus = nextStatus === 'approved' ? 'visible' : 'denied';
 122  			const batch = options.db.batch();
 123  			const attendeeRef = options.db.collection('events').doc(targetRequest.eventId).collection('attendees').doc(targetRequest.requestedByUid);
 124  			const requesterAttendeeSnapshot = nextStatus === 'approved'
 125  				? await attendeeRef.get()
 126  				: null;
 127  			const inviteSnapshot = await options.db.collection('eventInvites')
 128  				.where('eventId', '==', targetRequest.eventId)
 129  				.get();
 130  
 131  			batch.update(options.db.collection('bookingRequests').doc(targetRequest.id), {
 132  				status: nextStatus,
 133  				decidedByUid: options.actor.uid,
 134  				decidedByName: actorDisplayName,
 135  				decidedAt: options.serverTimestamp()
 136  			});
 137  
 138  			batch.update(options.db.collection('events').doc(targetRequest.eventId), {
 139  				status: nextEventStatus
 140  			});
 141  
 142  			inviteSnapshot.docs.forEach((doc) => {
 143  				batch.update(doc.ref, {
 144  					eventStatus: nextEventStatus,
 145  					updatedAt: options.serverTimestamp()
 146  				});
 147  			});
 148  
 149  			if (nextStatus === 'approved' && !requesterAttendeeSnapshot?.exists) {
 150  				batch.set(attendeeRef, {
 151  					uid: targetRequest.requestedByUid,
 152  					displayName: targetRequest.requesterName || 'Resident',
 153  					joinedAt: options.serverTimestamp()
 154  				});
 155  			}
 156  
 157  			// Keep each request/event pair in its own batch so approval plus same-day
 158  			// auto-declines do not trip the Firestore rules expression limit.
 159  			await batch.commit();
 160  
 161  			updatedRequests.push({
 162  				id: targetRequest.id,
 163  				status: nextStatus,
 164  				decidedByUid: options.actor.uid,
 165  				decidedByName: actorDisplayName,
 166  				eventId: targetRequest.eventId,
 167  				eventStatus: nextEventStatus
 168  			});
 169  		}
 170  
 171  		await applyDecision(request, decision);
 172  
 173  		if (decision === 'approved') {
 174  			for (const siblingRequest of siblingRequests) {
 175  				if (siblingRequest.id === request.id || siblingRequest.status !== 'pending') {
 176  					continue;
 177  				}
 178  
 179  				await applyDecision(siblingRequest, 'denied');
 180  			}
 181  		}
 182  
 183  		return {
 184  			requestStatus: decision,
 185  			eventStatus: decision === 'approved' ? 'visible' : 'denied',
 186  			updatedRequests: updatedRequests
 187  		};
 188  	}

Hendelsesflyt

4. Brukeren åpner hendelsesdetaljer

Detaljer rutes inne i dashboard-workspace, ikke på en separat serverside. Valgt hendelses-id driver henting av deltakere og rendering av detalj-overlay.
