firestore.rules:1189-1233regler for events/{eventId}/attendees/{attendeeId}
// firestore.rules:1189-1233
1189        match /attendees/{userId} {
1190          // Stores simple membership records for the residents who joined a specific
1191          // event.
1192          // Attendees belong naturally under one event, so the membership list stays
1193          // close to the parent record that most screens already load.
1194          // Allows reads only when the parent event itself is readable.
1195          // Attendee records reveal participation, so they follow the same access
1196          // boundary as the event they belong to.
1197          allow read: if canAccessEventDocument(
1198            get(/databases/$(database)/documents/events/$(eventId)).data
1199          );
1200          // Allows residents to create only their own attendee document, with one
1201          // extra reviewer path for approved room-booking flows.
1202          // This prevents one resident from silently joining another user to an
1203          // event while still supporting the booking approval workflow.
1204          allow create: if isSignedIn()
1205            && request.resource.data.uid == userId
1206            && request.resource.data.displayName is string
1207            && (
1208              (
1209                request.auth.uid == userId
1210                && (
1211                  canAccessVisibleEvent(
1212                    getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/events/$(eventId)).data
1213                  )
1214                  || (
1215                    getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/events/$(eventId)).data.status in ['visible', 'pending']
1216                    && getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/events/$(eventId)).data.createdByUid == request.auth.uid
1217                  )
1218                )
1219              )
1220              || reviewerCanCreateApprovedBookingAttendee(eventId, userId)
1221            );
1222          // Allows either the attendee or the event owner to remove membership.
1223          // Leaving an event should stay self-service, while hosts still need a way
1224          // to remove attendees from their own event.
1225          allow delete: if isSignedIn()
1226            && (
1227              request.auth.uid == userId
1228              || get(/databases/$(database)/documents/events/$(eventId)).data.createdByUid == request.auth.uid
1229            );
1230          // Blocks attendee document edits.
1231          // Membership records are intentionally simple, so updates are replaced by
1232          // create or delete flows instead of free-form editing.
1233          allow update: if false;
leser events/{eventId}/attendees
Hvorfor dette betyr noe: Detaljer kan ikke vise korrekt RSVP-/bli med-status hvis tilgang til deltaker-subcollection ikke fungerer.

Dataflyt

deltakerdokumenter blir deltakerchips og tilstand for medlemsknapper.

eventInvites markerer invite-only-synlighet og inviterte brukere.

 152  	// Small helper for setting text on optional DOM elements.
 153  	function setElementText(element, text) {
 154  		if (!element) {
 155  			return;
 156  		}
 157  
 158  		element.textContent = text;
 159  	}

 161  	// Small helper for keeping `hidden`, `aria-hidden`, and the helper class in sync.
 162  	function setElementHidden(element, isHidden) {
 163  		if (!element) {
 164  			return;
 165  		}
 166  
 167  		element.hidden = Boolean(isHidden);
 168  		element.toggleAttribute('hidden', Boolean(isHidden));
 169  		element.setAttribute('aria-hidden', isHidden ? 'true' : 'false');
 170  		element.classList.toggle('is-visible', !isHidden);
 171  		if (isHidden) {
 172  			element.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
 173  		} else {
 174  			element.style.removeProperty('display');
 175  		}
 176  	}

 179  	// Status copy is centralized here so all event-details surfaces describe the same
 180  	// workflow state with the same badge vocabulary.
 181  	const STATUS_VIEWS = {
 182  		approved: {
 183  			label: 'Approved',
 184  			badgeClass: 'approved'
 185  		},
 186  		pending: {
 187  			label: 'Pending approval',
 188  			badgeClass: 'pending'
 189  		},
 190  		declined: {
 191  			label: 'Declined',
 192  			badgeClass: 'declined'
 193  		},
 194  		canceled: {
 195  			label: 'Canceled',
 196  			badgeClass: 'canceled'
 197  		}
 198  	};
 199

 201  		return statusView.badgeClass;

 204  		// Keep event-details feedback rendering in one helper.
 205  	function setFeedback(options) {
 206  		const feedbackElement = options.feedbackElement;
 207  		const message = options.message || '';
 208  		const isError = Boolean(options.isError);
 209  
 210  		if (!feedbackElement) {
 211  			return;
 212  		}
 213  
 214  		feedbackElement.textContent = message;
 215  		feedbackElement.dataset.state = message ? (isError ? 'error' : 'success') : '';
 216  	}

 218  	// Normalize the different status spellings used across older and newer event flows.
 219  	function normalizeStatus(status) {
 220  		const rawStatus = String(status || '').toLowerCase();
 221  
 222  		if (rawStatus === 'approved' || rawStatus === 'visible') {
 223  			return 'approved';
 224  		}
 225  
 226  		if (rawStatus === 'pending') {
 227  			return 'pending';
 228  		}
 229  
 230  		if (rawStatus === 'denied' || rawStatus === 'declined') {
 231  			return 'declined';
 232  		}
 233  
 234  		if (rawStatus === 'canceled') {
 235  			return 'canceled';
 236  		}
 237  
 238  		console.warn('event-details-ui: unrecognised status value:', status);
 239  		return 'pending';
 240  	}
 241  
 242  	// Return the badge configuration for one normalized status.
 243  	function getStatusView(status) {
 244  		const normalizedStatus = normalizeStatus(status);
 245  
 246  		return STATUS_VIEWS[normalizedStatus] || STATUS_VIEWS.pending;
 247  	}

public/scripts/ui-parts/event-details-ui.js:274-336renderhjelpere for deltakere
// public/scripts/ui-parts/event-details-ui.js:274-336
 274  	// Render the attendee chips and count block for the event details view.
 275  	// Participant visibility depends on event status and privacy, so the count and
 276  	// chip rendering stay together in one helper instead of being split across the
 277  	// main details renderer.
 278  	function renderParticipants(options) {
 279  		const elements = options.elements || {};
 280  		const attendees = Array.isArray(options.attendees) ? options.attendees : [];
 281  		const hostUid = String(options.hostUid || '');
 282  		const isCanceled = Boolean(options.isCanceled);
 283  		const isPrivate = Boolean(options.isPrivate);
 284  		const hideForStatus = Boolean(options.hideForStatus);
 285  		const useCommonRoom = Boolean(options.useCommonRoom);
 286  
 287  		if (!elements.participantsElement || !elements.participantsListElement || !elements.participantsCountElement) {
 288  			return;
 289  		}
 290  
 291  		elements.participantsElement.hidden = isCanceled || isPrivate || hideForStatus;
 292  		elements.participantsListElement.innerHTML = '';
 293  
 294  		if (isCanceled) {
 295  			elements.participantsCountElement.textContent = '';
 296  			return;
 297  		}
 298  
 299  		// Common room has a fixed capacity of 40; other events show only the count
 300  		const cap = window.COMMON_ROOM_CONFIG?.maxParticipants ?? 40;
 301  		elements.participantsCountElement.textContent = useCommonRoom
 302  			? `${attendees.length} / ${cap}`
 303  			: String(attendees.length);
 304  
 305  		attendees.forEach(function (attendee) {
 306  			const item = document.createElement('div');
 307  			const avatarWrap = document.createElement('div');
 308  			const avatar = document.createElement('div');
 309  			const name = document.createElement('div');
 310  			const displayName = String(attendee.displayName || 'Unknown participant').trim();
 311  			const firstName = displayName.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)[0] || displayName;
 312  			const isHost = hostUid && String(attendee.uid || '') === hostUid;
 313  
 314  			item.className = isHost ? 'participant-chip participant-chip-host' : 'participant-chip';
 315  			avatarWrap.className = 'participant-avatar-wrap';
 316  			avatar.className = 'participant-avatar';
 317  			avatar.textContent = avatarHelpers.getInitials(displayName);
 318  			var avatarColors = avatarHelpers.buildAvatarColors(`${attendee.displayName || ''}-${attendee.uid || ''}`);
 319  			avatar.style.background = avatarColors.bg;
 320  			avatar.style.color = avatarColors.fg;
 321  			name.className = 'participant-name';
 322  			name.textContent = firstName;
 323  
 324  			if (isHost) {
 325  				const crown = document.createElement('i');
 326  				crown.className = 'participant-host-crown ti ti-crown';
 327  				crown.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
 328  				avatarWrap.appendChild(crown);
 329  			}
 330  
 331  			avatarWrap.appendChild(avatar);
 332  			item.appendChild(avatarWrap);
 333  			item.appendChild(name);
 334  			elements.participantsListElement.appendChild(item);
 335  		});
 336  	}

 511  	// Show or hide the menu actions depending on ownership, edit mode, and event status.
 512  	function renderActionButtons(elements, isOwnEvent, isEditing, normalizedStatus) {
 513  		const isCanceled = normalizedStatus === 'canceled';
 514  		const isDeclined = normalizedStatus === 'declined';
 515  		const canEdit = isOwnEvent && !isCanceled && !isDeclined;
 516  
 517  		if (elements.menuButtonElement) {
 518  			setElementHidden(elements.menuButtonElement, isEditing || !isOwnEvent);
 519  			elements.menuButtonElement.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
 520  		}
 521  		if (elements.menuElement) {
 522  			setElementHidden(elements.menuElement, true);
 523  		}
 524  		if (elements.menuEditSectionElement) {
 525  			setElementHidden(elements.menuEditSectionElement, isEditing || !canEdit);
 526  		}
 527  		if (elements.editButtonElement) {
 528  			setElementHidden(elements.editButtonElement, isEditing || !canEdit);
 529  		}
 530  		if (elements.cancelButtonElement) {
 531  			setElementHidden(elements.cancelButtonElement, isEditing || !isOwnEvent || normalizedStatus !== 'approved');
 532  		}
 533  		if (elements.deleteButtonElement) {
 534  			setElementHidden(elements.deleteButtonElement, isEditing || !isOwnEvent);
 535  		}
 536  	}

 538  	// Update the join/leave button based on membership state and event status.
 539  	function renderMembershipButton(elements, isOwnEvent, isJoinedEvent, isCanceled, justJoined, isPendingInvite) {
 540  		if (!elements.membershipButtonElement) {
 541  			return;
 542  		}
 543  		setElementHidden(elements.membershipButtonElement, isCanceled || isOwnEvent);
 544  		elements.membershipButtonElement.setAttribute('aria-pressed', isJoinedEvent ? 'true' : 'false');
 545  		elements.membershipButtonElement.classList.toggle('is-joined', isJoinedEvent);
 546  		elements.membershipButtonElement.classList.toggle('is-not-joined', !isJoinedEvent);
 547  		elements.membershipButtonElement.classList.toggle('is-canceled', isCanceled);
 548  
 549  		const icon = elements.membershipButtonElement.querySelector('#event-membership-icon');
 550  		const label = elements.membershipButtonElement.querySelector('#event-membership-label');
 551  		if (icon) {
 552  			icon.classList.toggle('ti-calendar-minus', isJoinedEvent);
 553  			icon.classList.toggle('ti-calendar-plus', !isJoinedEvent);
 554  		}
 555  		if (label) {
 556  			label.textContent = isJoinedEvent
 557  				? 'Leave event'
 558  				: (isPendingInvite ? 'Accept invite' : 'Join event');
 559  		}
 560  		if (justJoined) {
 561  			elements.membershipButtonElement.classList.add('is-pop');
 562  			setTimeout(function () {
 563  				elements.membershipButtonElement.classList.remove('is-pop');
 564  			}, 420);
 565  			burstConfetti();
 566  		}
 567  	}
public/scripts/ui-parts/event-details-ui.js:569-673renderDetails() bygger overlay-innholdet
// public/scripts/ui-parts/event-details-ui.js:569-673
 569  	// Render the full event details view from the current event state.
 570  	// This is the main view renderer for the overlay. It translates the raw event,
 571  	// membership, and edit state into the smaller rendering helpers above so the
 572  	// page layer does not need to know each individual DOM rule.
 573  	function renderDetails(options) {
 574  		const elements = options.elements || {};
 575  		const event = options.event;
 576  		const isOwnEvent = Boolean(options.isOwnEvent);
 577  		const isJoinedEvent = Boolean(options.isJoinedEvent);
 578  		const isEditing = Boolean(options.isEditing);
 579  		const editDraft = options.editDraft || null;
 580  		const normalizedStatus = normalizeStatus(event.status);
 581  		const statusView = getStatusView(normalizedStatus);
 582  		const statusMessageView = getStatusMessageView(event, normalizedStatus, isOwnEvent);
 583  		const isCanceled = normalizedStatus === 'canceled';
 584  		const eventCardElement = elements.titleElement?.closest('.event-details-card') || null;
 585  		const audienceView = window.connectData?.events?.describeEventAudience
 586  			? window.connectData.events.describeEventAudience(event)
 587  			: { badge: 'Everyone', detail: 'Everyone' };
 588  
 589  		const eventTimeSummary = event.endTime
 590  			? `${event.startTime || '--:--'} to ${event.endTime}`
 591  			: `Starts at ${event.startTime || '--:--'}`;
 592  
 593  		setElementText(elements.titleElement, event.name);
 594  		setElementText(elements.hostNameElement, event.createdByName || 'Unknown');
 595  		setElementText(
 596  			elements.visibilityTileElement,
 597  			audienceView.badge
 598  		);
 599  
 600  		if (elements.dateElement) {
 601  			setElementText(elements.dateElement, event.eventDate || '');
 602  		}
 603  
 604  		renderDateBadge(event.eventDate, isCanceled);
 605  
 606  		setElementText(elements.timeElement, eventTimeSummary);
 607  		setElementText(elements.timeRangeElement, `${event.startTime || '--:--'} - ${event.endTime || '--:--'}`);
 608  		setElementText(elements.descriptionElement, event.description || 'No description provided.');
 609  
 610  		renderLocationTile(elements.locationElement, event);
 611  
 612  		if (elements.statusMessageBlockElement) {
 613  			setElementHidden(elements.statusMessageBlockElement, !statusMessageView.visible);
 614  		}
 615  		if (elements.statusMessageTitleElement) {
 616  			setElementText(elements.statusMessageTitleElement, statusMessageView.title);
 617  		}
 618  		if (elements.statusMessageReasonElement) {
 619  			setElementText(elements.statusMessageReasonElement, statusMessageView.reason);
 620  		}
 621  		if (elements.statusElement) {
 622  			setElementHidden(elements.statusElement, !isOwnEvent);
 623  			if (isOwnEvent) {
 624  				elements.statusElement.classList.remove(...STATUS_BADGE_CLASSES);
 625  				elements.statusElement.classList.add(statusView.badgeClass);
 626  				setElementText(elements.statusElement, statusView.label);
 627  			}
 628  		}
 629  		if (elements.viewModeElement) {
 630  			setElementHidden(elements.viewModeElement, isEditing);
 631  		}
 632  		if (elements.editModeElement) {
 633  			setElementHidden(elements.editModeElement, !isEditing);
 634  		}
 635  
 636  		renderEditFields(elements, event, isEditing, editDraft);
 637  		renderActionButtons(elements, isOwnEvent, isEditing, normalizedStatus);
 638  
 639  		if (elements.visibilityElement) {
 640  			setElementText(elements.visibilityElement, audienceView.detail);
 641  		}
 642  
 643  		renderMembershipButton(elements, isOwnEvent, isJoinedEvent, isCanceled, options.justJoined, Boolean(options.isPendingInvite));
 644  
 645  		if (eventCardElement) {
 646  			eventCardElement.classList.toggle('event-details-canceled', isCanceled);
 647  			eventCardElement.classList.toggle('event-details-declined', normalizedStatus === 'declined');
 648  			eventCardElement.classList.toggle('event-details-editing', isEditing);
 649  			eventCardElement.classList.toggle('event-details-private', Boolean(event.isPrivate));
 650  		}
 651  
 652  		renderParticipants({
 653  			elements,
 654  			attendees: options.attendees,
 655  			hostUid: event.createdByUid,
 656  			isCanceled,
 657  			isPrivate: Boolean(event.isPrivate),
 658  			hideForStatus: normalizedStatus === 'pending' || normalizedStatus === 'declined',
 659  			useCommonRoom: Boolean(event.useCommonRoom)
 660  		});

 663  			setElementText(elements.createdAtElement, `Created ${options.formatCreatedAt(event.createdAt)}`);
 664  		}
 665  		if (elements.updatedAtElement && typeof options.formatCreatedAt === 'function') {
 666  			const updated = event.updatedAt || null;
 667  			if (updated && String(updated) !== String(event.createdAt)) {
 668  				setElementText(elements.updatedAtElement, `Updated ${options.formatCreatedAt(updated)}`);
 669  			} else {

4.4 Live-oppdateringer holder åpen detaljvisning oppdatert

① Trigger: Firestore-snapshots kaller `refreshDashboardWorkspace()`, deretter bruker workspace valgt hendelsestilstand på nytt.
② Kjeden:
public/scripts/features/dashboard/dashboard-workspace.js:1245-1455snapshot-callbacks fra subscribeToEventUpdates()
// public/scripts/features/dashboard/dashboard-workspace.js:1245-1455
1245      function subscribeToEventUpdates(options) {
1246        const { db, user, userProfile, onChanged } = options;
1247  
1248        let debounceTimer = null;
1249      const visibleSnapshots = new Map();
1250      let ownEventsById = new Map();
1251      let inviteRecordsById = new Map();
1252      const inviteEventDocsById = new Map();
1253      const inviteEventUnsubs = new Map();
1254  
1255      function syncInviteEventSubscriptions() {
1256        const inviteVisibleEventIds = new Set([...inviteRecordsById.values()].reduce(function (result, inviteRecord) {
1257          const eventId = String(inviteRecord?.eventId || '');
1258          if (inviteRecord?.audienceMode === 'inviteOnly' && inviteRecord?.eventStatus === 'visible' && eventId) {
1259            result.push(eventId);
1260          }
1261          return result;
1262        }, []));
1263  
1264        inviteEventUnsubs.forEach(function (unsubscribe, eventId) {
1265          if (inviteVisibleEventIds.has(eventId)) {
1266            return;
1267          }
1268  
1269          unsubscribe();
1270          inviteEventUnsubs.delete(eventId);
1271          inviteEventDocsById.delete(eventId);
1272        });
1273  
1274        inviteVisibleEventIds.forEach(function (eventId) {
1275          if (inviteEventUnsubs.has(eventId)) {
1276            return;
1277          }
1278  
1279          const unsubscribe = db.collection('events').doc(eventId).onSnapshot(function (snapshot) {
1280            if (snapshot.exists) {
1281              inviteEventDocsById.set(eventId, eventDataApi.normalizeEventRecord({ id: snapshot.id, ...snapshot.data() }));
1282            } else {
1283              inviteEventDocsById.delete(eventId);
1284            }
1285            scheduleRefresh();
1286          }, function () {
1287            inviteEventDocsById.delete(eventId);
1288            scheduleRefresh();
1289          });
1290  
1291          inviteEventUnsubs.set(eventId, unsubscribe);
1292        });
1293      }
1294  
1295      function buildLiveEventCollections() {
1296        const rawVisibleEvents = [...visibleSnapshots.values()].flatMap(function (snapshot) {
1297          return snapshot.docs.map(function (doc) {
1298            return eventDataApi.normalizeEventRecord({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() });
1299          });
1300        });
1301        const invitedEvents = eventDataApi.buildUniqueEventList([...inviteEventDocsById.values()]).filter(function (event) {
1302          return eventDataApi.canUserAccessVisibleEvent(event, {
1303            userId: user.uid,
1304            userProfile
1305          });
1306        });
1307        const visibleEvents = eventDataApi.buildUniqueEventList(rawVisibleEvents.concat(invitedEvents)).filter(function (event) {
1308          return eventDataApi.canUserAccessVisibleEvent(event, {
1309            userId: user.uid,
1310            userProfile
1311          });
1312        });
1313        const ownEvents = eventDataApi.sortEventsByDateTime(eventDataApi.buildUniqueEventList([
1314          ...ownEventsById.values(),
1315          ...optimisticOwnEventsById.values()
1316        ]));
1317        const joinedEvents = eventDataApi.sortEventsByDateTime(visibleEvents.filter(function (event) {
1318          return joinedEventIds.has(event.id);
1319        }));
1320  
1321        return {
1322          visibleEvents,
1323          ownEvents,
1324          joinedEventIds: new Set(joinedEventIds),
1325          joinedEvents,
1326          calendarEvents: eventDataApi.buildUniqueEventList([...ownEvents, ...joinedEvents, ...invitedEvents]),
1327          inviteRecords: [...inviteRecordsById.values()]
1328        };
1329      }
1330  
1331        function scheduleRefresh() {
1332          clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
1333        debounceTimer = setTimeout(function () {
1334          const eventCollections = buildLiveEventCollections();
1335          applyEventCollections(eventCollections);
1336  
1337          if (typeof onChanged === 'function') {
1338            Promise.resolve(onChanged({
1339              visibleEvents: eventCollections.visibleEvents,
1340              ownEvents: eventCollections.ownEvents,
1341              joinedEvents: eventCollections.joinedEvents,
1342              inviteRecords: eventCollections.inviteRecords
1343            })).catch(function (error) {
1344              console.error('dashboard event subscription refresh failed:', error);
1345            });
1346          }
1347          }, 120);
1348        }
1349  
1350        const visibleUnsubs = (eventDataApi.buildAccessibleVisibleEventQueries({
1351          db,
1352          user,
1353          userProfile
1354      }) || []).map(function (query, index) {
1355        return query.onSnapshot(function (snapshot) {
1356          visibleSnapshots.set(index, snapshot);
1357          scheduleRefresh();
1358        }, function () {});
1359      });
1360  
1361        const unsubOwn = db.collection('events')
1362          .where('createdByUid', '==', user.uid)
1363  		.onSnapshot(function (snapshot) {
1364          ownEventsById = new Map();
1365          snapshot.docs.forEach(function (doc) {
1366            const rawOwnEvent = { id: doc.id, ...doc.data() };
1367            ownEventsById.set(doc.id, eventDataApi.normalizeEventRecord(rawOwnEvent));
1368  				optimisticOwnEventsById.delete(doc.id);
1369          });
1370          scheduleRefresh();
1371        }, function () {});
1372      const unsubInvites = typeof eventDataApi.subscribeUserEventInvites === 'function'
1373        ? eventDataApi.subscribeUserEventInvites({
1374          db,
1375          userId: user.uid,
1376          onChange: function (inviteRecords) {
1377            inviteRecordsById = new Map();
1378            inviteRecords.forEach(function (inviteRecord) {
1379              inviteRecordsById.set(inviteRecord.id, inviteRecord);
1380            });
1381            syncInviteEventSubscriptions();
1382            scheduleRefresh();
1383          },
1384          onError: function () {}
1385        })
1386        : null;
1387  
1388        return function unsubscribe() {
1389          clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
1390          visibleUnsubs.forEach((unsubscribe) => unsubscribe());
1391          unsubOwn();
1392        if (typeof unsubInvites === 'function') {
1393          unsubInvites();
1394        }
1395        inviteEventUnsubs.forEach(function (unsubscribe) {
1396          unsubscribe();
1397        });
1398        inviteEventUnsubs.clear();
1399        inviteEventDocsById.clear();
1400        };
1401      }
1402  
1403      function resetWorkspace() {
1404        joinedEventIds = new Set();
1405        currentVisibleEvents = [];
1406        currentOwnEvents = [];
1407        currentJoinedEvents = [];
1408    		currentInviteRecords = [];
1409  		optimisticOwnEventsById = new Map();

Hendelsesflyt

5. Brukeren redigerer en hendelse

Redigering finnes i to lag: en montert redigeringsskjemakontroller og inline-redigering i dashboard-detaljene. Begge kaller til slutt `updateEventDetails()`.

 567  	}
kaller eventsEdit.startEdit(event) eller workspace.startInlineEdit()
Hvorfor dette betyr noe: UI-handlingen må bære nøyaktig valgt hendelse inn i redigeringstilstand slik at skjemaet kan forhåndsutfylles.

Dataflyt

valgt hendelsesobjekt blir redigerbare draft-verdier.

canEdit styrer om redigeringsstien er tilgjengelig.

 267  		// Enter edit mode and prefill the form from the selected event.
 268      // The event-details overlay can stay mounted while the selected event changes, so
 269      // entering edit mode always repopulates the fields from the latest selected record.
 270      async function startEdit() {

1054      function startInlineEdit() {
1055        const selectedEventContext = getOwnedSelectedEventContext();
1056  
1057        if (!selectedEventContext) {
1058          return false;
1059        }
1060  
1061        const selectedEvent = selectedEventContext.selectedEvent;
1062

1064        inlineEditState.draft = {
1065          name: String(selectedEvent.name || ''),
1066          description: String(selectedEvent.description || ''),
1067          eventDate: String(selectedEvent.eventDate || ''),
1068          startTime: String(selectedEvent.startTime || ''),
1069          endTime: String(selectedEvent.endTime || ''),
1070          locationMode: selectedEvent.useCommonRoom ? 'commonRoom' : 'custom',
1071          audienceMode: String(selectedEvent.audienceMode || 'anyone'),
1072          location: selectedEvent.useCommonRoom ? '' : String(selectedEvent.location || '')
1073        };
1074        renderSelectedEventDetails();
1075        return true;
1076      }
1077  
1078      function cancelInlineEdit() {
1079        if (!inlineEditState.active) {
1080          return false;
1081        }
1082  
1083        clearInlineEditState();
1084        showEventDetailsFeedback('');
1085        renderSelectedEventDetails();
1086        return true;
1087      }
1088  
1089      async function saveInlineEdit(payload, onChanged) {
1090        const selectedEventContext = getOwnedSelectedEventContext({ requireProfile: true });
1091  
1092        if (!inlineEditState.active || !selectedEventContext) {
1093          return false;
1094        }
1095  
1096        const selectedEvent = selectedEventContext.selectedEvent;
1097        const currentProfile = selectedEventContext.currentProfile;
1098  
1099        const name = String(payload?.name || '').trim();
1100        const description = String(payload?.description || '').trim();
1101        const eventDateLocked = Boolean(selectedEvent.useCommonRoom);
1102        const eventDate = eventDateLocked
1103          ? String(selectedEvent.eventDate || '').trim()
1104          : String(payload?.eventDate || '').trim();
1105        const startTime = String(payload?.startTime || '').trim();
1106        const endTime = String(payload?.endTime || '').trim();
1107        const useCommonRoom = Boolean(selectedEvent.useCommonRoom);
1108        const location = useCommonRoom ? '' : String(payload?.location || '').trim();
1109        const audienceMode = String(payload?.audienceMode || 'anyone');
1110        const extraInviteTargets = audienceMode === 'anyone' || !Array.isArray(payload?.extraInviteTargets)
1111          ? []
1112          : payload.extraInviteTargets;
1113  
1114        if (!name) {
1115          throw new Error('Event name is required.');
1116        }
1117        if (!eventDate) {
1118          throw new Error('Date is required.');
1119        }
1120        if (!startTime || !endTime) {
1121          throw new Error('Start and end time are required.');
1122        }
1123        if (startTime >= endTime) {
1124          throw new Error('End time must be after start time.');
1125        }
1126        if (!useCommonRoom && !location) {
1127          throw new Error('Custom location is required.');
1128        }
1129  
1130        await eventDataApi.updateEventDetails({
1131          db,
1132          serverTimestamp,
1133          eventId: selectedEvent.id,
1134          bookingRequestId: selectedEvent.bookingRequestId || '',
1135          currentStatus: selectedEvent.status,
1136          name,
1137          description,
1138          eventDate,
1139          startTime,
1140          endTime,
1141          useCommonRoom,
1142          actorProfile: currentProfile,
1143          audienceMode,
1144          extraInviteTargets,
1145          location
1146        });
1147  
1148        clearInlineEditState();
1149  
1150        if (typeof onChanged === 'function') {
1151          await onChanged();
1152        }
1153  
1154        showEventDetailsFeedback('Event updated.');
1155        await renderSelectedEventDetails();
1156        return true;
1157      }
forhåndsutfyller tittel/dato/tid/beskrivelse/målgruppe/invitasjoner/romfelt

5.3 Lagre validerer og bygger update-payload

② Kjeden:
public/scripts/features/events/events-edit.js:321-377saveEdit() validerer og kaller updateEventDetails()
// public/scripts/features/events/events-edit.js:321-377
 321  		// Validate and save the edited event values.
 322      // Validation stays in this controller because the edit overlay combines shared
 323      // form rules with event-specific decisions like locked common-room dates and
 324      // private invite-only confirmations.
 325      async function saveEdit() {
 326        try {
 327          if (!ui.editNameElement?.value.trim()) {
 328            throw new Error('Event title is required.');
 329          }
 330          if (!ui.editDescriptionElement?.value.trim()) {
 331            throw new Error('Description is required.');
 332          }
 333          const selectedEvent = getSelectedEvent();
 334          const nextTitle = String(ui.editNameElement?.value || '').trim();
 335          const currentTitle = String(selectedEvent?.name || '').trim();
 336  
 337          if (nextTitle.length > MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH && nextTitle !== currentTitle) {
 338            throw new Error(`Event title must be ${MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH} characters or fewer.`);
 339          }
 340  
 341          const dateLocked = Boolean(selectedEvent?.useCommonRoom);
 342          const nextDate = dateLocked
 343            ? (selectedEvent?.eventDate || '')
 344            : (ui.editDateElement?.value || '');
 345          const locationMode = selectedEvent?.useCommonRoom
 346            ? 'commonRoom'
 347            : (ui.editLocationModeElement?.value || 'custom');
 348  
 349          if (selectedEvent?.useCommonRoom && nextDate && approvedCommonRoomDates.has(nextDate) && nextDate !== selectedEvent?.eventDate) {
 350            throw new Error('The common room is already booked on that date.');
 351          }
 352  
 353          const audienceMode = ui.editVisibilityElement?.value || 'anyone';
 354          const extraInviteTargets = audienceMode === 'anyone' ? [] : (visibilityCombobox?.getTargets() || []);
 355  
 356          if (audienceMode === 'inviteOnly' && extraInviteTargets.length === 0) {
 357            const keepPrivate = window.confirm('This will save the event as a private solo event that only you can see. Continue?');
 358            if (!keepPrivate) {
 359              return;
 360            }
 361          }
 362  
 363          await onSaveEdit({
 364            name: ui.editNameElement?.value,
 365            description: ui.editDescriptionElement?.value,
 366            eventDate: nextDate,
 367            startTime: getTimeValue('from'),
 368            endTime: getTimeValue('to'),
 369            locationMode,
 370            audienceMode,
 371            extraInviteTargets,
 372            location: ui.editLocationElement?.value
 373          }, onAfterSave);
 374        } catch (error) {
 375          onError(error.message);
 376        }
 377      }
public/scripts/features/events/events-edit.js:379-445bindListeners() kobler knapper og skjemahendelser

 379  		// Bind the edit-mode DOM listeners once.
 380      // Listener wiring lives in one place so the event-details page can create the
 381      // controller once and then drive edit mode through the returned API.
 382      function bindListeners() {
 383        ui.editStartElement?.addEventListener('click', () => openTimePicker('from'));
 384        ui.editEndElement?.addEventListener('click', () => openTimePicker('to'));
 385  
 386        ui.editCancelElement?.addEventListener('click', () => cancelEdit());
 387        ui.editSaveElement?.addEventListener('click', () => saveEdit());
 388  
 389        ui.editLocationModeElement?.addEventListener('change', () => {
 390          if (ui.editLocationRowElement) {
 391            ui.editLocationRowElement.hidden = isCommonRoomMode();
 392          }
 393          syncFormControls();
 394          buildCalendar();
 395        });
 396  
 397        ui.editDateButtonElement?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
 398          if (isDateLocked()) {
 399            return;
 400          }
 401          await loadApprovedCommonRoomDates();
 402          if (ui.editCalendarElement) {
 403            ui.editCalendarElement.hidden = !ui.editCalendarElement.hidden;
 404            ui.editScheduleGridElement?.classList.toggle('open', !ui.editCalendarElement.hidden);
 405            if (!ui.editCalendarElement.hidden) {

 407                anchorElement: ui.editDateButtonElement,
 408                panelElement: ui.editCalendarElement,
 409                containerElement: ui.editScheduleGridElement,
 410                width: 264
 411              });
 412            }
 413            buildCalendar();
 414          }
 415        });
 416  
 417        ui.editCalendarPrevElement?.addEventListener('click', () => {
 418          calendarMonth -= 1;
 419          if (calendarMonth < 0) {
 420            calendarMonth = 11;
 421            calendarYear -= 1;
 422          }
 423          buildCalendar();
 424        });
 425  
 426        ui.editCalendarNextElement?.addEventListener('click', () => {
 427          calendarMonth += 1;
 428          if (calendarMonth > 11) {
 429            calendarMonth = 0;
 430            calendarYear += 1;
 431          }
 432          buildCalendar();
 433        });
 434  
 435        ui.editVisibilityElement?.addEventListener('change', () => {
 436          const audienceMode = ui.editVisibilityElement.value || 'anyone';
 437          syncVisibilityAudienceMode(audienceMode);
 438          if (audienceMode === 'anyone') {
 439            visibilityCombobox?.setTargets([]);
 440          } else {
 441            ui.editVisibilitySearchElement?.focus();
 442          }
 443          syncFormControls();
 444        });
 445      }
public/scripts/ui-parts/event-form-ui.js:391-470delte skjemavalideringsregler
// public/scripts/ui-parts/event-form-ui.js:391-470
 391  	// Validate the create/edit form values before a write is attempted.
 392  	// The validation order mirrors the user-facing form so the first visible errors
 393  	// still match the sections the user just interacted with.
 394  	function validateForm(options) {
 395  		let isValid = true;
 396  		const values = options.values;
 397  		const elements = options.elements;
 398  		const trimmedName = values.name.trim();
 399  
 400  		if (!trimmedName) {
 401  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 402  				errorElement: elements.nameErrorElement,
 403  				message: 'Event title is required.',
 404  				invalidElements: [elements.nameFieldElement]
 405  			}) && isValid;
 406  		} else if (trimmedName.length > MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH) {
 407  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 408  				errorElement: elements.nameErrorElement,
 409  				message: `Event title must be ${MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH} characters or fewer.`,
 410  				invalidElements: [elements.nameFieldElement]
 411  			}) && isValid;
 412  		}
 413  
 414  		if (!values.description.trim()) {
 415  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 416  				errorElement: elements.descriptionErrorElement,
 417  				message: 'Description is required.',
 418  				invalidElements: [elements.descriptionInputElement]
 419  			}) && isValid;
 420  		}
 421  
 422  		if (values.requiresCustomLocation && !values.location.trim()) {
 423  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 424  				errorElement: elements.locationErrorElement,
 425  				message: 'Location is required.',
 426  				applyInvalidState: function () {
 427  					setLocationFieldInvalidState(true);
 428  				}
 429  			}) && isValid;
 430  		}
 431  
 432  		if (!values.date) {
 433  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 434  				errorElement: elements.dateErrorElement,
 435  				message: 'Select a date.'
 436  			}) && isValid;
 437  		} else if (values.isPastDate) {
 438  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 439  				errorElement: elements.dateErrorElement,
 440  				message: 'Past dates cannot be selected.'
 441  			}) && isValid;
 442  		} else if (values.isCommonRoom && values.isBusyDate) {
 443  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 444  				errorElement: elements.dateErrorElement,
 445  				message: 'This day is already booked.'
 446  			}) && isValid;
 447  		}
 448  
 449  		if (!values.timeFrom || !values.timeTo) {
 450  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 451  				errorElement: elements.timeErrorElement,
 452  				message: 'Select a start and end time.'
 453  			}) && isValid;
 454  		} else if (options.toMinutes(values.timeTo) <= options.toMinutes(values.timeFrom)) {
 455  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 456  				errorElement: elements.timeErrorElement,
 457  				message: 'End time must be after start time.'
 458  			}) && isValid;
 459  		}
 460  
 461  		if (values.isCommonRoom && !values.acceptedTerms) {
 462  			isValid = reportValidationFailure({
 463  				errorElement: elements.termsErrorElement,
 464  				message: 'Accept the common-room terms to continue.',
 465  				invalidElements: [elements.termsCheckboxElement]
 466  			}) && isValid;
 467  		}
 468  
 469  		return isValid;
 470  	}

// public/scripts/data/events-data.js:912-1008
 912  	// Update the editable fields for an event and refresh any invite documents that
 913  	// depend on those fields.
 914  	async function updateEventDetails(options) {
 915  		const eventRef = options.db.collection('events').doc(options.eventId);
 916  		const eventSnapshot = await eventRef.get();
 917  		const existingEvent = eventSnapshot.exists
 918  			? normalizeEventRecord({ id: eventSnapshot.id, ...eventSnapshot.data() })
 919  			: null;
 920  		const nextName = validateEventTitle(options.name, {
 921  			previousTitle: existingEvent?.name,
 922  			allowUnchangedLegacyTitle: true
 923  		});
 924  		const useCommonRoom = Boolean(options.useCommonRoom);
 925  		const updateData = useCommonRoom
 926  			? {
 927  				name: nextName,
 928  				description: String(options.description || '').trim(),
 929  				startTime: String(options.startTime || ''),
 930  				endTime: String(options.endTime || ''),
 931  				updatedAt: options.serverTimestamp()
 932  			}
 933  			: (() => {
 934  				const audienceFields = buildAudienceFields({
 935  					audienceMode: options.audienceMode,
 936  					actorProfile: options.actorProfile,
 937  					extraInviteTargets: options.extraInviteTargets
 938  				});
 939  
 940  				return {
 941  					name: nextName,
 942  					description: String(options.description || '').trim(),
 943  					eventDate: String(options.eventDate || ''),
 944  					startTime: String(options.startTime || ''),
 945  					endTime: String(options.endTime || ''),
 946  					useCommonRoom,
 947  					location: String(options.location || '').trim() || null,
 948  					isPrivate: audienceFields.isPrivate,
 949  					audienceMode: audienceFields.audienceMode,
 950  					audienceScopeValue: audienceFields.audienceScopeValue,
 951  					extraInviteUids: audienceFields.extraInviteUids,
 952  					visibilityTargets: audienceFields.visibilityTargets,
 953  					status: options.currentStatus || 'visible',
 954  					updatedAt: options.serverTimestamp()
 955  				};
 956  			})();
 957  
 958  		if (useCommonRoom && !options.bookingRequestId) {
 959  			throw new Error('Common-room events must stay linked to a booking request. Refresh and try again.');
 960  		}
 961  
 962  		const nextEventData = normalizeEventRecord({
 963  			...(existingEvent || {}),
 964  			id: options.eventId,
 965  			...updateData
 966  		});
 967  		const existingInvites = await loadEventInviteRecordsFromEvent({
 968  			db: options.db,
 969  			eventId: options.eventId,
 970  			eventData: existingEvent || {}
 971  		});
 972  
 973  		if (useCommonRoom && options.bookingRequestId) {
 974  			const batch = options.db.batch();
 975  			batch.update(eventRef, updateData);
 976  			batch.update(options.db.collection('bookingRequests').doc(options.bookingRequestId), {
 977  				eventName: updateData.name,
 978  				startTime: updateData.startTime,
 979  				endTime: updateData.endTime || ''
 980  			});
 981  			await syncEventInviteDocuments({
 982  				db: options.db,
 983  				batch,
 984  				eventId: options.eventId,
 985  				eventData: nextEventData,
 986  				existingInvites,
 987  				serverTimestamp: options.serverTimestamp
 988  			});
 989  			await batch.commit();
 990  		} else {
 991  			const batch = options.db.batch();
 992  			batch.update(eventRef, updateData);
 993  			await syncEventInviteDocuments({
 994  				db: options.db,
 995  				batch,

 568  	// Build or refresh the stored invite document payload for one invited resident.
 569  	function buildEventInviteRecordData(options) {
 570  		const normalizedEvent = normalizeEventRecord(options.eventData || {});
 571  		const inviteTarget = sanitizeResidentInviteTarget(options.inviteTarget);
 572  		const existingInvite = normalizeEventInviteRecord(options.existingInvite || {});
 573  		const responseStatus = existingInvite?.responseStatus === INVITE_RESPONSE_ACCEPTED
 574  			? INVITE_RESPONSE_ACCEPTED
 575  			: INVITE_RESPONSE_PENDING;

 578  			return null;
 579  		}

 581  		return {
 582  			eventId: sanitizeString(options.eventId),
 583  			invitedUid: inviteTarget.value,
 584  			invitedName: inviteTarget.label,
 585  			createdByUid: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.createdByUid),
 586  			createdByName: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.createdByName || 'Resident'),
 587  			eventName: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.name || 'Untitled event'),
 588  			eventDate: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.eventDate),
 589  			startTime: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.startTime),
 590  			endTime: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.endTime),
 591  			eventStatus: sanitizeString(normalizedEvent.status || 'visible'),
 592  			audienceMode: sanitizeAudienceMode(normalizedEvent.audienceMode) || 'inviteOnly',
 593  			responseStatus,
 594  			createdAt: existingInvite?.createdAt || options.serverTimestamp(),
 595  			updatedAt: options.serverTimestamp(),
 596  			respondedAt: responseStatus === INVITE_RESPONSE_ACCEPTED
 597  				? (existingInvite?.respondedAt || null)
 598  				: null
 599  		};
 600  	}

 629  	async function loadEventInviteRecordsFromEvent(options) {
 630  		const eventId = sanitizeString(options.eventId || options.eventData?.id);
 631  		const inviteTargets = getResidentInviteTargetsForEvent(options.eventData || {});
 632  
 633  		if (!eventId || !inviteTargets.length) {
 634  			return [];
 635  		}
 636  
 637  		const inviteSnapshots = await Promise.all(inviteTargets.map(async (inviteTarget) => {
 638  			try {
 639  				const snapshot = await getEventInvitesCollection(options.db)
 640  					.doc(buildEventInviteDocId(eventId, inviteTarget.value))
 641  					.get();
 642  
 643  				return snapshot.exists
 644  					? normalizeEventInviteRecord({
 645  						id: snapshot.id,
 646  						ref: snapshot.ref,
 647  						...snapshot.data()
 648  					})
 649  					: null;
 650  			} catch (error) {
 651  				console.warn('Failed to load event invite document:', eventId, inviteTarget.value, error);
 652  				return null;
 653  			}
 654  		}));
 655  
 656  		return inviteSnapshots.filter(Boolean);
 657  	}

 660  	function subscribeUserEventInvites(options) {
 661  		return getEventInvitesCollection(options.db)
 662  			.where('invitedUid', '==', sanitizeString(options.userId))
 663  			.onSnapshot((snapshot) => {
 664  				const inviteRecords = snapshot.docs.map((doc) => normalizeEventInviteRecord({
 665  					id: doc.id,
 666  					ref: doc.ref,
 667  					...doc.data()
 668  				}));
 669  
 670  				if (typeof options.onChange === 'function') {
 671  					options.onChange(inviteRecords);
 672  				}
 673  			}, (error) => {
 674  				if (typeof options.onError === 'function') {
 675  					options.onError(error);
 676  				}
 677  			});
 678  	}

 680  	// Make the event invite documents match the event's current extra-invite targets.
 681  	async function syncEventInviteDocuments(options) {
 682  		const inviteTargets = getResidentInviteTargetsForEvent(options.eventData || {});
 683  		const existingInvites = Array.isArray(options.existingInvites)
 684  			? options.existingInvites.map((invite) => normalizeEventInviteRecord(invite))
 685  			: await listEventInviteRecordsForEvent({
 686  				db: options.db,
 687  				eventId: options.eventId
 688  			});
 689  		const existingByUid = new Map();
 690  		const batch = options.batch || options.db.batch();
 691  
 692  		existingInvites.forEach((invite) => {
 693  			existingByUid.set(invite.invitedUid, invite);
 694  		});
 695  
 696  		const nextInvitedUids = new Set(inviteTargets.map((target) => target.value));
 697  
 698  		existingInvites.forEach((invite) => {
 699  			if (!nextInvitedUids.has(invite.invitedUid) && invite.ref) {
 700  				batch.delete(invite.ref);
 701  			}
 702  		});
 703  
 704  		inviteTargets.forEach((inviteTarget) => {
 705  			const inviteData = buildEventInviteRecordData({
 706  				eventId: options.eventId,
 707  				eventData: options.eventData,
 708  				inviteTarget,
 709  				existingInvite: existingByUid.get(inviteTarget.value),
 710  				serverTimestamp: options.serverTimestamp
 711  			});
 712  
 713  			if (!inviteData) {
 714  				return;
 715  			}
 716  
 717  			batch.set(
 718  				getEventInvitesCollection(options.db).doc(buildEventInviteDocId(options.eventId, inviteTarget.value)),
 719  				inviteData
 720  			);
 721  		});
 722  
 723  		if (!options.batch) {
 724  			await batch.commit();
 725  		}
 726  	}
firestore.rules:612-654ownerCanEditEventDetails()
// firestore.rules:612-654
 612      // Allows event owners to edit the safe subset of fields for visible or pending
 613      // events.
 614      // Normal custom events and common-room bookings follow different workflows, so
 615      // the editable fields stay narrower once room approval is involved.
 616      function ownerCanEditEventDetails() {
 617        return isSignedIn()
 618          && resource.data.createdByUid == request.auth.uid
 619          && resource.data.status in ['visible', 'pending']
 620          && eventOwnerFieldsAreUnchanged()
 621          && eventUseCommonRoomIsUnchanged()
 622          && eventStatusIsUnchanged()
 623          && eventTitleUpdateIsAllowed()
 624          && isValidEventAudienceState(request.resource.data)
 625          && request.resource.data.description is string
 626          && request.resource.data.startTime is string
 627          && request.resource.data.endTime is string
 628          && (
 629            (
 630              // Allows owners to update the date, location, and audience settings on
 631              // normal custom-location events.
 632              // These events have no linked room review record, so those fields can
 633              // stay flexible without breaking moderation data.
 634              resource.data.useCommonRoom == false
 635              && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
 636                'name', 'description', 'location', 'eventDate',
 637  			      'startTime', 'endTime', 'audienceMode', 'audienceScopeValue', 'extraInviteUids',
 638  			      'visibilityTargets', 'isPrivate', 'updatedAt'
 639              ])
 640              && request.resource.data.eventDate is string
 641              && (request.resource.data.location == null || request.resource.data.location is string)
 642            )
 643            || (
 644              // Limits common-room edits to the name, description, and time fields.
 645              // Once a room request exists, the date and audience must stay locked so
 646              // reviewers are still judging the same request.
 647              resource.data.useCommonRoom == true
 648              && request.resource.data.eventDate == resource.data.eventDate
 649              && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
 650                'name', 'description', 'startTime', 'endTime', 'audienceScopeValue', 'updatedAt'
 651              ])
 652            )
 653          );
 654      }

// public/scripts/features/events/events-edit.js:321-377
 321  		// Validate and save the edited event values.
 322      // Validation stays in this controller because the edit overlay combines shared
 323      // form rules with event-specific decisions like locked common-room dates and
 324      // private invite-only confirmations.
 325      async function saveEdit() {
 326        try {
 327          if (!ui.editNameElement?.value.trim()) {
 328            throw new Error('Event title is required.');
 329          }
 330          if (!ui.editDescriptionElement?.value.trim()) {
 331            throw new Error('Description is required.');
 332          }
 333          const selectedEvent = getSelectedEvent();
 334          const nextTitle = String(ui.editNameElement?.value || '').trim();
 335          const currentTitle = String(selectedEvent?.name || '').trim();
 336  
 337          if (nextTitle.length > MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH && nextTitle !== currentTitle) {
 338            throw new Error(`Event title must be ${MAX_EVENT_TITLE_LENGTH} characters or fewer.`);
 339          }
 340  
 341          const dateLocked = Boolean(selectedEvent?.useCommonRoom);
 342          const nextDate = dateLocked
 343            ? (selectedEvent?.eventDate || '')
 344            : (ui.editDateElement?.value || '');
 345          const locationMode = selectedEvent?.useCommonRoom
 346            ? 'commonRoom'
 347            : (ui.editLocationModeElement?.value || 'custom');
 348  
 349          if (selectedEvent?.useCommonRoom && nextDate && approvedCommonRoomDates.has(nextDate) && nextDate !== selectedEvent?.eventDate) {
 350            throw new Error('The common room is already booked on that date.');
 351          }
 352  
 353          const audienceMode = ui.editVisibilityElement?.value || 'anyone';
 354          const extraInviteTargets = audienceMode === 'anyone' ? [] : (visibilityCombobox?.getTargets() || []);
 355  
 356          if (audienceMode === 'inviteOnly' && extraInviteTargets.length === 0) {
 357            const keepPrivate = window.confirm('This will save the event as a private solo event that only you can see. Continue?');
 358            if (!keepPrivate) {
 359              return;
 360            }
 361          }
 362  
 363          await onSaveEdit({
 364            name: ui.editNameElement?.value,
 365            description: ui.editDescriptionElement?.value,
 366            eventDate: nextDate,
 367            startTime: getTimeValue('from'),
 368            endTime: getTimeValue('to'),
 369            locationMode,
 370            audienceMode,
 371            extraInviteTargets,
 372            location: ui.editLocationElement?.value
 373          }, onAfterSave);
 374        } catch (error) {
 375          onError(error.message);
 376        }
 377      }

 617  		}

 619  			setElementText(elements.statusMessageReasonElement, statusMessageView.reason);

Dataflyt

oppdatert hendelses-snapshot erstatter gammel valgt hendelse.

redigeringstilstand ryddes slik at read-only-detaljer rendres igjen.

Hendelsesflyt

6. Brukeren sletter en hendelse

Sletting er en destruktiv eiersti: detaljhandling -> bekreftelse -> datahjelper -> Firestore-sletting -> lokal opprydding/live re-render.

6.1 Slettehandling vises bare når det er tillatt

① Trigger: Hendelsesdetalj-overlayet bestemmer om slettehandlinger skal vises.
② Kjeden:
public/scripts/ui-parts/event-details-ui.js:511-567regler for slettehandling og knapperendering
// public/scripts/ui-parts/event-details-ui.js:511-567
 511  	// Show or hide the menu actions depending on ownership, edit mode, and event status.
 512  	function renderActionButtons(elements, isOwnEvent, isEditing, normalizedStatus) {
 513  		const isCanceled = normalizedStatus === 'canceled';
 514  		const isDeclined = normalizedStatus === 'declined';
 515  		const canEdit = isOwnEvent && !isCanceled && !isDeclined;
 516  
 517  		if (elements.menuButtonElement) {
 518  			setElementHidden(elements.menuButtonElement, isEditing || !isOwnEvent);
 519  			elements.menuButtonElement.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
 520  		}
 521  		if (elements.menuElement) {
 522  			setElementHidden(elements.menuElement, true);
 523  		}
 524  		if (elements.menuEditSectionElement) {
 525  			setElementHidden(elements.menuEditSectionElement, isEditing || !canEdit);
 526  		}
 527  		if (elements.editButtonElement) {

 539  	function renderMembershipButton(elements, isOwnEvent, isJoinedEvent, isCanceled, justJoined, isPendingInvite) {
 540  		if (!elements.membershipButtonElement) {
 541  			return;
 542  		}
 543  		setElementHidden(elements.membershipButtonElement, isCanceled || isOwnEvent);
 544  		elements.membershipButtonElement.setAttribute('aria-pressed', isJoinedEvent ? 'true' : 'false');

 547  		elements.membershipButtonElement.classList.toggle('is-canceled', isCanceled);
 548

 550  		const label = elements.membershipButtonElement.querySelector('#event-membership-label');
 551  		if (icon) {
 552  			icon.classList.toggle('ti-calendar-minus', isJoinedEvent);
 553  			icon.classList.toggle('ti-calendar-plus', !isJoinedEvent);
 554  		}
 555  		if (label) {
 556  			label.textContent = isJoinedEvent
 557  				? 'Leave event'
 558  				: (isPendingInvite ? 'Accept invite' : 'Join event');
 559  		}
 560  		if (justJoined) {
 561  			elements.membershipButtonElement.classList.add('is-pop');
 562  			setTimeout(function () {
 563  				elements.membershipButtonElement.classList.remove('is-pop');
 564  			}, 420);
 565  			burstConfetti();
 566  		}
 567  	}

public/scripts/features/dashboard/dashboard-workspace.js:1200-1231deleteSelectedEvent()
// public/scripts/features/dashboard/dashboard-workspace.js:1200-1231
1200      async function deleteSelectedEvent(onChanged) {
1201        if (membershipChangeInFlight) {
1202          return false;
1203        }
1204  
1205        const selectedEventContext = getOwnedSelectedEventContext();
1206  
1207        if (!selectedEventContext) {
1208          return false;
1209        }
1210  
1211        const selectedEvent = selectedEventContext.selectedEvent;
1212  
1213        membershipChangeInFlight = true;
1214        setMembershipButtonBusy(true);
1215  
1216        try {
1217          await eventDataApi.deleteEvent({
1218            db,
1219            eventId: selectedEvent.id
1220          });
1221  
1222          if (typeof onChanged === 'function') {
1223            await onChanged();
1224          }
1225  
1226          return true;
1227        } finally {
1228          membershipChangeInFlight = false;
1229          setMembershipButtonBusy(false);
1230        }
1231      }

6.3 Datahjelper sletter hendelsen og koblede dokumenter

① Trigger: `deleteSelectedEvent()` delegerer til `deleteEvent()`.
② Kjeden:
public/scripts/data/events-data.js:871-910deleteEvent()
// public/scripts/data/events-data.js:871-910
 871  	// Delete an event, its attendee subcollection, its invite docs, and any linked
 872  	// booking request that should disappear with it.
 873  	async function deleteEvent(options) {
 874  		const eventRef = options.db.collection('events').doc(options.eventId);
 875  		const eventSnapshot = await eventRef.get();
 876  		const eventData = eventSnapshot.exists ? eventSnapshot.data() : {};
 877  		const attendeesSnapshot = await eventRef.collection('attendees').get();
 878  		const inviteRecords = await loadEventInviteRecordsFromEvent({
 879  			db: options.db,
 880  			eventId: options.eventId,
 881  			eventData: eventSnapshot.exists ? { id: eventSnapshot.id, ...eventSnapshot.data() } : {}
 882  		});
 883  		const maxBatchWrites = 450;
 884  
 885  		for (let index = 0; index < attendeesSnapshot.docs.length; index += maxBatchWrites) {
 886  			const attendeeBatch = options.db.batch();
 887  			attendeesSnapshot.docs.slice(index, index + maxBatchWrites).forEach((doc) => {
 888  				attendeeBatch.delete(doc.ref);
 889  			});
 890  			await attendeeBatch.commit();
 891  		}
 892  
 893  		const batch = options.db.batch();
 894  		if (eventData.bookingRequestId && ['pending', 'approved', 'denied', 'canceled', 'visible'].includes(String(eventData.status || '').toLowerCase())) {
 895  			batch.delete(options.db.collection('bookingRequests').doc(eventData.bookingRequestId));
 896  		}
 897  		inviteRecords.forEach((inviteRecord) => {
 898  			if (inviteRecord.ref) {
 899  				batch.delete(inviteRecord.ref);
 900  			}
 901  		});
 902  		batch.delete(eventRef);
 903  
 904  		await batch.commit();
 905  
 906  		return {
 907  			eventId: options.eventId,
 908  			deleted: true
 909  		};
 910  	}
firestore.rules:691-708hjelper for eiersletting / opprydding av koblet hendelsesbestilling
// firestore.rules:691-708
 691      // Allows event owners to delete their own events.
 692      // The delete path stays narrow because ownership is the only stable reason to
 693      // remove the whole event document.
 694      function ownerCanDeleteEvent() {
 695        return isSignedIn()
 696          && resource.data.createdByUid == request.auth.uid;
 697      }
 698  
 699      // Checks that deleting a booking request also deletes its linked event in the
 700      // same write flow.
 701      // Common-room booking requests should not be left behind without the matching
 702      // event document they were created to support.
 703      function ownerDeletesLinkedEventForBookingRequest(requestId) {
 704        return resource.data.keys().hasAny(['eventId'])
 705          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/events/$(resource.data.eventId))
 706          && !existsAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/events/$(resource.data.eventId))
 707          && get(/databases/$(database)/documents/events/$(resource.data.eventId)).data.createdByUid == request.auth.uid
 708          && get(/databases/$(database)/documents/events/$(resource.data.eventId)).data.bookingRequestId == requestId;
firestore.rules:1100-1187sletteregel for events/{eventId}
// firestore.rules:1100-1187
1100      match /events/{eventId} {
1101        // Stores the main event documents shown in the calendar, profile views, and
1102        // event detail panels.
1103        // Events sit at the center of several features, so the base document has to
1104        // start in one normalized shape that later helpers can trust.
1105        // Creates new events only for the signed-in resident and only with the full
1106        // normalized audience fields already present.
1107        // Requiring the final audience shape from the first write keeps later reads,
1108        // edits, and invite syncing much simpler.
1109        allow create: if isResidentEmail()
1110          && request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly([
1111            'name',
1112            'description',
1113            'eventDate',
1114            'startTime',
1115            'endTime',
1116            'useCommonRoom',
1117            'location',
1118            'audienceMode',
1119            'audienceScopeValue',
1120            'extraInviteUids',
1121            'isPrivate',
1122            'visibilityTargets',
1123            'status',
1124            'createdByUid',
1125            'createdByName',
1126            'createdAt',
1127            'bookingRequestId'
1128          ])
1129          && request.resource.data.createdByUid == request.auth.uid
1130          && request.resource.data.createdByName is string
1131          && request.resource.data.createdByName.size() > 0
1132          && request.resource.data.createdByName.size() <= 120
1133          && request.resource.data.createdAt is timestamp
1134          && request.resource.data.useCommonRoom is bool
1135          && isValidEventTitle(request.resource.data.name)
1136          && request.resource.data.description is string
1137          && request.resource.data.description.size() <= 2000
1138          && request.resource.data.eventDate is string
1139          && request.resource.data.startTime is string
1140          && request.resource.data.endTime is string
1141          && (request.resource.data.location == null || request.resource.data.location is string)
1142          && isValidEventAudienceState(request.resource.data)
1143          && (
1144            (request.resource.data.useCommonRoom == false
1145              && request.resource.data.status == 'visible'
1146              && !request.resource.data.keys().hasAny(['bookingRequestId']))
1147            ||
1148            (request.resource.data.useCommonRoom == true
1149              && request.resource.data.status == 'pending'
1150              && request.resource.data.bookingRequestId is string)
1151          );
1152  
1153        // Allows reads for residents who can access the event plus the staff roles
1154        // that need moderation visibility.
1155        // Event browsing is a shared feature, but private and pending records still
1156        // need one central gate instead of separate read rules everywhere.
1157        allow read: if canAccessEventDocument(resource.data);
1158  
1159        // Allows owner edits, owner cancellations, and reviewer approval decisions on
1160        // the narrow paths defined by the helpers above.
1161        // Event updates carry the most workflow risk in this file, so each allowed
1162        // branch is kept separate and tied to matching booking-request changes.
1163        allow update: if !invalidRenamedEventTitle()
1164          && (
1165            ownerCanEditEventDetails()
1166            || ownerCanCancelEvent()
1167            || (
1168              hasBookingReviewAccess()
1169              && resource.data.useCommonRoom == true
1170              && resource.data.status == 'pending'
1171              && request.resource.data.status in ['visible', 'denied']
1172              && isSameEventShape()
1173              && decisionMatchesEventStatus(
1174                getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/bookingRequests/$(resource.data.bookingRequestId)).data.status,
1175                request.resource.data.status
1176              )
1177              && getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/bookingRequests/$(resource.data.bookingRequestId)).data.decidedByUid == request.auth.uid
1178              && (
1179                getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/bookingRequests/$(resource.data.bookingRequestId)).data.requestedByUid != request.auth.uid
1180                || getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/bookingRequests/$(resource.data.bookingRequestId)).data.status == 'denied'
1181              )
1182            )
1183          );
1184        // Allows the owner to delete the event through the dedicated helper.
1185        // Delete access stays separate from normal edits so ownership remains the one
1186        // clear reason an event can disappear.
1187        allow delete: if ownerCanDeleteEvent();
sletter Firestore-sti events/{eventId}
Hvorfor dette betyr noe: Å bare slette det synlige kortet ville etterlate den kanoniske hendelsen i Firestore, og den ville dukke opp igjen ved neste snapshot.

Dataflyt

request: slett dokumentet ved `events/{eventId}` pluss hjelpeopprydding der det er implementert.

respons: ingen body; suksess vises ved at Firestore fullfører og snapshots ikke lenger inneholder hendelsen.

 927    function applyEventCollections(eventCollections) {
 928      currentVisibleEvents = Array.isArray(eventCollections?.visibleEvents) ? eventCollections.visibleEvents : [];
 929      currentOwnEvents = Array.isArray(eventCollections?.ownEvents) ? eventCollections.ownEvents : [];
 930  		currentOwnEvents.forEach(function (event) {
 931  			if (event?.id) {
 932  				optimisticOwnEventsById.delete(event.id);
 933  			}
 934  		});
 935      joinedEventIds = eventCollections?.joinedEventIds instanceof Set
 936        ? new Set(eventCollections.joinedEventIds)
 937        : new Set(Array.isArray(eventCollections?.joinedEventIds) ? eventCollections.joinedEventIds : []);
 938      currentJoinedEvents = Array.isArray(eventCollections?.joinedEvents) ? eventCollections.joinedEvents : [];
 939      currentInviteRecords = Array.isArray(eventCollections?.inviteRecords) ? eventCollections.inviteRecords : [];

 944      function applyCreatedOwnEvent(submitResult) {
 945        if (!submitResult?.eventId || !submitResult.event) {
 946          return null;
 947        }
 948  
 949        // Mirror the just-created event into the local dashboard state immediately so
 950        // the creator can see it even if the listener refresh lands a moment later.
 951        const createdEvent = eventDataApi.normalizeEventRecord({
 952          id: submitResult.eventId,
 953          ...submitResult.event
 954        });
 955  		optimisticOwnEventsById.set(createdEvent.id, createdEvent);
 956  
 957        currentOwnEvents = eventDataApi.buildUniqueEventList([
 958          ...currentOwnEvents,
 959          createdEvent
 960        ]);
 961        renderCurrentCalendarEvents();
 962  
 963        if (selectedExpandedCalendarDate && selectedExpandedCalendarDate === createdEvent.eventDate) {
 964          restoreExpandedCalendarDayPopup();
 965        }
 966  
 967        return createdEvent;
 968      }

Hendelsesflyt

7. Brukeren avlyser en hendelse

Avlysning er annerledes enn sletting: dokumentet blir liggende, men status og avlysningsmetadata endres. Da kan historikk, invitasjonstilstand og koblet bestillingskontekst overleve.

 161  	// Small helper for keeping `hidden`, `aria-hidden`, and the helper class in sync.
 162  	function setElementHidden(element, isHidden) {
 163  		if (!element) {
 164  			return;
 165  		}
 166  
 167  		element.hidden = Boolean(isHidden);
 168  		element.toggleAttribute('hidden', Boolean(isHidden));
 169  		element.setAttribute('aria-hidden', isHidden ? 'true' : 'false');
 170  		element.classList.toggle('is-visible', !isHidden);
 171  		if (isHidden) {
 172  			element.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
 173  		} else {
 174  			element.style.removeProperty('display');
 175  		}
 176  	}

 178  	// Mapping from normalized event status to the badge copy and styling used in the overlay.
 179  	// Status copy is centralized here so all event-details surfaces describe the same
 180  	// workflow state with the same badge vocabulary.
 181  	const STATUS_VIEWS = {
 182  		approved: {
 183  			label: 'Approved',
 184  			badgeClass: 'approved'
 185  		},
 186  		pending: {
 187  			label: 'Pending approval',
 188  			badgeClass: 'pending'
 189  		},
 190  		declined: {
 191  			label: 'Declined',
 192  			badgeClass: 'declined'
 193  		},

 196  			badgeClass: 'canceled'
 197  		}

 201  		return statusView.badgeClass;

 204  		// Keep event-details feedback rendering in one helper.
 205  	function setFeedback(options) {
 206  		const feedbackElement = options.feedbackElement;
 207  		const message = options.message || '';
 208  		const isError = Boolean(options.isError);
 209  
 210  		if (!feedbackElement) {
 211  			return;
 212  		}
 213  
 214  		feedbackElement.textContent = message;
 215  		feedbackElement.dataset.state = message ? (isError ? 'error' : 'success') : '';
 216  	}

 218  	// Normalize the different status spellings used across older and newer event flows.
 219  	function normalizeStatus(status) {
 220  		const rawStatus = String(status || '').toLowerCase();
 221  
 222  		if (rawStatus === 'approved' || rawStatus === 'visible') {
 223  			return 'approved';
 224  		}
 225  
 226  		if (rawStatus === 'pending') {
 227  			return 'pending';
 228  		}
 229  
 230  		if (rawStatus === 'denied' || rawStatus === 'declined') {
 231  			return 'declined';
 232  		}
 233  
 234  		if (rawStatus === 'canceled') {
 235  			return 'canceled';
 236  		}
 237  
 238  		console.warn('event-details-ui: unrecognised status value:', status);
 239  		return 'pending';
 240  	}
 241  
 242  	// Return the badge configuration for one normalized status.
 243  	function getStatusView(status) {
 244  		const normalizedStatus = normalizeStatus(status);
 245  
 246  		return STATUS_VIEWS[normalizedStatus] || STATUS_VIEWS.pending;
 247  	}

 512  	function renderActionButtons(elements, isOwnEvent, isEditing, normalizedStatus) {

 539  	function renderMembershipButton(elements, isOwnEvent, isJoinedEvent, isCanceled, justJoined, isPendingInvite) {

 559  		}

 562  			setTimeout(function () {
 563  				elements.membershipButtonElement.classList.remove('is-pop');
 564  			}, 420);
 565  			burstConfetti();
 566  		}
 567  	}

1159      async function cancelSelectedEvent(cancellationReason, onChanged) {

1176          await eventDataApi.updateEventStatus({
1177            db,
1178            serverTimestamp,
1179            eventId: selectedEvent.id,
1180            status: 'canceled',
1181            cancellationReason
1182          });

1185          selectedEvent.cancellationReason = cancellationReason;
1186

1188            await onChanged();
1189          }
1190

1192          await renderSelectedEventDetails();
1193          return true;
1194        } finally {
1195          membershipChangeInFlight = false;
1196          setMembershipButtonBusy(false);
1197        }

 830  	// Update one event's status and keep any invite documents in sync with that status.
 831  	async function updateEventStatus(options) {
 832  		const eventRef = options.db.collection('events').doc(options.eventId);
 833  		const eventSnapshot = await eventRef.get();
 834  		const updateData = {
 835  			status: options.status,
 836  			updatedAt: options.serverTimestamp()
 837  		};
 838  
 839  		if (options.status === 'canceled' && options.cancellationReason) {
 840  			updateData.cancellationReason = options.cancellationReason;
 841  		}
 842  
 843  		const batch = options.db.batch();
 844  		batch.update(eventRef, updateData);
 845  		const currentEvent = eventSnapshot.exists
 846  			? normalizeEventRecord({ id: eventSnapshot.id, ...eventSnapshot.data(), ...updateData })
 847  			: normalizeEventRecord({ id: options.eventId, ...updateData });
 848  		const existingInvites = await loadEventInviteRecordsFromEvent({
 849  			db: options.db,
 850  			eventId: options.eventId,
 851  			eventData: eventSnapshot.exists ? { id: eventSnapshot.id, ...eventSnapshot.data() } : {}
 852  		});
 853  
 854  		await syncEventInviteDocuments({
 855  			db: options.db,
 856  			batch,
 857  			eventId: options.eventId,
 858  			eventData: currentEvent,
 859  			existingInvites,
 860  			serverTimestamp: options.serverTimestamp
 861  		});
 862  
 863  		await batch.commit();
 864  
 865  		return {
 866  			eventId: options.eventId,
 867  			status: options.status
 868  		};
 869  	}

// firestore.rules:1100-1187
1100      match /events/{eventId} {
1101        // Stores the main event documents shown in the calendar, profile views, and
1102        // event detail panels.
1103        // Events sit at the center of several features, so the base document has to
1104        // start in one normalized shape that later helpers can trust.
1105        // Creates new events only for the signed-in resident and only with the full
1106        // normalized audience fields already present.
1107        // Requiring the final audience shape from the first write keeps later reads,
1108        // edits, and invite syncing much simpler.
1109        allow create: if isResidentEmail()
1110          && request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly([
1111            'name',
1112            'description',
1113            'eventDate',
1114            'startTime',
1115            'endTime',
1116            'useCommonRoom',
1117            'location',
1118            'audienceMode',
1119            'audienceScopeValue',
1120            'extraInviteUids',
1121            'isPrivate',
1122            'visibilityTargets',
1123            'status',
1124            'createdByUid',
1125            'createdByName',
1126            'createdAt',
1127            'bookingRequestId'
1128          ])
1129          && request.resource.data.createdByUid == request.auth.uid
1130          && request.resource.data.createdByName is string
1131          && request.resource.data.createdByName.size() > 0
1132          && request.resource.data.createdByName.size() <= 120
1133          && request.resource.data.createdAt is timestamp
1134          && request.resource.data.useCommonRoom is bool

1136          && request.resource.data.description is string
1137          && request.resource.data.description.size() <= 2000
1138          && request.resource.data.eventDate is string
1139          && request.resource.data.startTime is string
1140          && request.resource.data.endTime is string
1141          && (request.resource.data.location == null || request.resource.data.location is string)

1143          && (
1144            (request.resource.data.useCommonRoom == false
1145              && request.resource.data.status == 'visible'
1146              && !request.resource.data.keys().hasAny(['bookingRequestId']))
1147            ||
1148            (request.resource.data.useCommonRoom == true
1149              && request.resource.data.status == 'pending'
1150              && request.resource.data.bookingRequestId is string)
1151          );
1152  
1153        // Allows reads for residents who can access the event plus the staff roles
1154        // that need moderation visibility.
1155        // Event browsing is a shared feature, but private and pending records still
1156        // need one central gate instead of separate read rules everywhere.
1157        allow read: if canAccessEventDocument(resource.data);
1158  
1159        // Allows owner edits, owner cancellations, and reviewer approval decisions on
1160        // the narrow paths defined by the helpers above.
1161        // Event updates carry the most workflow risk in this file, so each allowed
1162        // branch is kept separate and tied to matching booking-request changes.
1163        allow update: if !invalidRenamedEventTitle()

1176              )

1181              )
1182            )
1183          );
1184        // Allows the owner to delete the event through the dedicated helper.
1185        // Delete access stays separate from normal edits so ownership remains the one
1186        // clear reason an event can disappear.
1187        allow delete: if ownerCanDeleteEvent();

 944      function applyCreatedOwnEvent(submitResult) {
 945        if (!submitResult?.eventId || !submitResult.event) {
 946          return null;
 947        }
 948  
 949        // Mirror the just-created event into the local dashboard state immediately so
 950        // the creator can see it even if the listener refresh lands a moment later.
 951        const createdEvent = eventDataApi.normalizeEventRecord({
 952          id: submitResult.eventId,
 953          ...submitResult.event
 954        });
 955  		optimisticOwnEventsById.set(createdEvent.id, createdEvent);
 956

 958          ...currentOwnEvents,
 959          createdEvent
 960        ]);
 961        renderCurrentCalendarEvents();
 962  
 963        if (selectedExpandedCalendarDate && selectedExpandedCalendarDate === createdEvent.eventDate) {
 964          restoreExpandedCalendarDayPopup();
 965        }
 966  
 967        return createdEvent;
 968      }

 980        return {
 981          visibleEvents: eventCollections.visibleEvents,
 982          ownEvents: eventCollections.ownEvents,
 983          joinedEvents: eventCollections.joinedEvents
 984        };
 985      }
 986  
 987      async function refreshWorkspace(user, onLoaded) {
 988        const eventData = await loadDashboardEvents(user);
 989        if (typeof onLoaded === 'function') {
 990          await onLoaded(eventData);
 991        }
 992        return eventData;
 993      }

 137  	// Mount the event-details overlay into its page container if it is not already present.
 138  	// The overlay markup is mounted lazily so the page only pays the DOM cost once,
 139  	// while later opens can reuse the same structure and element ids.
 140  	function mountEventDetailsContent(options = {}) {
 141  		const mountElement = options.mountElement || document.getElementById('event-details-content-mount');
 142  
 143  		if (!mountElement || mountElement.querySelector('#event-details-main')) {
 144  			return;

 148  		mountElement.dataset.view = 'details';

 152  	// Small helper for setting text on optional DOM elements.
 153  	function setElementText(element, text) {
 154  		if (!element) {
 155  			return;
 156  		}
 157  
 158  		element.textContent = text;
 159  	}
 160  
 161  	// Small helper for keeping `hidden`, `aria-hidden`, and the helper class in sync.
 162  	function setElementHidden(element, isHidden) {
 163  		if (!element) {
 164  			return;
 165  		}
 166  
 167  		element.hidden = Boolean(isHidden);
 168  		element.toggleAttribute('hidden', Boolean(isHidden));
 169  		element.setAttribute('aria-hidden', isHidden ? 'true' : 'false');
 170  		element.classList.toggle('is-visible', !isHidden);
 171  		if (isHidden) {
 172  			element.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
 173  		} else {
 174  			element.style.removeProperty('display');
 175  		}
 176  	}
