 178  	// Mapping from normalized event status to the badge copy and styling used in the overlay.
 179  	// Status copy is centralized here so all event-details surfaces describe the same
 180  	// workflow state with the same badge vocabulary.
 181  	const STATUS_VIEWS = {
 182  		approved: {
 183  			label: 'Approved',
 184  			badgeClass: 'approved'
 185  		},
 186  		pending: {
 187  			label: 'Pending approval',
 188  			badgeClass: 'pending'
 189  		},
 190  		declined: {
 191  			label: 'Declined',
 192  			badgeClass: 'declined'
 193  		},
 194  		canceled: {
 195  			label: 'Canceled',
 196  			badgeClass: 'canceled'
 197  		}
 198  	};
 199  
 200  	const STATUS_BADGE_CLASSES = Object.values(STATUS_VIEWS).map(function (statusView) {
 201  		return statusView.badgeClass;
 202  	});
 203  
 204  		// Keep event-details feedback rendering in one helper.
 205  	function setFeedback(options) {
 206  		const feedbackElement = options.feedbackElement;
 207  		const message = options.message || '';
 208  		const isError = Boolean(options.isError);
 209  
 210  		if (!feedbackElement) {
 211  			return;
 212  		}
 213  
 214  		feedbackElement.textContent = message;
 215  		feedbackElement.dataset.state = message ? (isError ? 'error' : 'success') : '';
 216  	}
 217  
 218  	// Normalize the different status spellings used across older and newer event flows.
 219  	function normalizeStatus(status) {
 220  		const rawStatus = String(status || '').toLowerCase();
 221  
 222  		if (rawStatus === 'approved' || rawStatus === 'visible') {
 223  			return 'approved';
 224  		}
 225  
 226  		if (rawStatus === 'pending') {
 227  			return 'pending';
 228  		}
 229  
 230  		if (rawStatus === 'denied' || rawStatus === 'declined') {
 231  			return 'declined';
 232  		}
 233  
 234  		if (rawStatus === 'canceled') {
 235  			return 'canceled';
 236  		}
 237  
 238  		console.warn('event-details-ui: unrecognised status value:', status);
 239  		return 'pending';
 240  	}
 241  
 242  	// Return the badge configuration for one normalized status.
 243  	function getStatusView(status) {
 244  		const normalizedStatus = normalizeStatus(status);
 245  
 246  		return STATUS_VIEWS[normalizedStatus] || STATUS_VIEWS.pending;
 247  	}
 248  
 249  	// Build the optional status message banner shown for canceled or declined events.
 250  	function getStatusMessageView(event, normalizedStatus, isOwnEvent) {

 254  				title: 'This event has been canceled',
 255  				reason: event.cancellationReason || 'No reason provided.'
 256  			};
 257  		}
 258  
 259  		if (normalizedStatus === 'declined' && isOwnEvent) {
 260  			return {
public/scripts/ui-parts/event-details-ui.js:569-673renderDetails() renderer detaljer for avlyst hendelse
// public/scripts/ui-parts/event-details-ui.js:569-673
 569  	// Render the full event details view from the current event state.
 570  	// This is the main view renderer for the overlay. It translates the raw event,
 571  	// membership, and edit state into the smaller rendering helpers above so the
 572  	// page layer does not need to know each individual DOM rule.
 573  	function renderDetails(options) {
 574  		const elements = options.elements || {};
 575  		const event = options.event;
 576  		const isOwnEvent = Boolean(options.isOwnEvent);
 577  		const isJoinedEvent = Boolean(options.isJoinedEvent);
 578  		const isEditing = Boolean(options.isEditing);
 579  		const editDraft = options.editDraft || null;
 580  		const normalizedStatus = normalizeStatus(event.status);
 581  		const statusView = getStatusView(normalizedStatus);
 582  		const statusMessageView = getStatusMessageView(event, normalizedStatus, isOwnEvent);
 583  		const isCanceled = normalizedStatus === 'canceled';
 584  		const eventCardElement = elements.titleElement?.closest('.event-details-card') || null;
 585  		const audienceView = window.connectData?.events?.describeEventAudience
 586  			? window.connectData.events.describeEventAudience(event)
 587  			: { badge: 'Everyone', detail: 'Everyone' };
 588  
 589  		const eventTimeSummary = event.endTime
 590  			? `${event.startTime || '--:--'} to ${event.endTime}`
 591  			: `Starts at ${event.startTime || '--:--'}`;
 592  
 593  		setElementText(elements.titleElement, event.name);
 594  		setElementText(elements.hostNameElement, event.createdByName || 'Unknown');
 595  		setElementText(
 596  			elements.visibilityTileElement,
 597  			audienceView.badge
 598  		);
 599  
 600  		if (elements.dateElement) {
 601  			setElementText(elements.dateElement, event.eventDate || '');
 602  		}
 603  
 604  		renderDateBadge(event.eventDate, isCanceled);
 605  
 606  		setElementText(elements.timeElement, eventTimeSummary);
 607  		setElementText(elements.timeRangeElement, `${event.startTime || '--:--'} - ${event.endTime || '--:--'}`);
 608  		setElementText(elements.descriptionElement, event.description || 'No description provided.');
 609  
 610  		renderLocationTile(elements.locationElement, event);
 611  
 612  		if (elements.statusMessageBlockElement) {
 613  			setElementHidden(elements.statusMessageBlockElement, !statusMessageView.visible);
 614  		}
 615  		if (elements.statusMessageTitleElement) {
 616  			setElementText(elements.statusMessageTitleElement, statusMessageView.title);
 617  		}
 618  		if (elements.statusMessageReasonElement) {
 619  			setElementText(elements.statusMessageReasonElement, statusMessageView.reason);
 620  		}
 621  		if (elements.statusElement) {
 622  			setElementHidden(elements.statusElement, !isOwnEvent);
 623  			if (isOwnEvent) {
 624  				elements.statusElement.classList.remove(...STATUS_BADGE_CLASSES);
 625  				elements.statusElement.classList.add(statusView.badgeClass);
 626  				setElementText(elements.statusElement, statusView.label);
 627  			}
 628  		}
 629  		if (elements.viewModeElement) {
 630  			setElementHidden(elements.viewModeElement, isEditing);
 631  		}
 632  		if (elements.editModeElement) {
 633  			setElementHidden(elements.editModeElement, !isEditing);
 634  		}
 635  
 636  		renderEditFields(elements, event, isEditing, editDraft);
 637  		renderActionButtons(elements, isOwnEvent, isEditing, normalizedStatus);
 638  
 639  		if (elements.visibilityElement) {
 640  			setElementText(elements.visibilityElement, audienceView.detail);
 641  		}
 642  
 643  		renderMembershipButton(elements, isOwnEvent, isJoinedEvent, isCanceled, options.justJoined, Boolean(options.isPendingInvite));
 644  
 645  		if (eventCardElement) {
 646  			eventCardElement.classList.toggle('event-details-canceled', isCanceled);
 647  			eventCardElement.classList.toggle('event-details-declined', normalizedStatus === 'declined');
 648  			eventCardElement.classList.toggle('event-details-editing', isEditing);
 649  			eventCardElement.classList.toggle('event-details-private', Boolean(event.isPrivate));
 650  		}
 651  
 652  		renderParticipants({
 653  			elements,
 654  			attendees: options.attendees,
 655  			hostUid: event.createdByUid,
 656  			isCanceled,
 657  			isPrivate: Boolean(event.isPrivate),
 658  			hideForStatus: normalizedStatus === 'pending' || normalizedStatus === 'declined',
 659  			useCommonRoom: Boolean(event.useCommonRoom)
 660  		});
 661  
 662  		if (elements.createdAtElement && typeof options.formatCreatedAt === 'function') {
 663  			setElementText(elements.createdAtElement, `Created ${options.formatCreatedAt(event.createdAt)}`);
 664  		}
 665  		if (elements.updatedAtElement && typeof options.formatCreatedAt === 'function') {
 666  			const updated = event.updatedAt || null;
 667  			if (updated && String(updated) !== String(event.createdAt)) {
 668  				setElementText(elements.updatedAtElement, `Updated ${options.formatCreatedAt(updated)}`);
 669  			} else {
 670  				setElementText(elements.updatedAtElement, '');
 671  			}
 672  		}
 673  	}
